# f5xc_http_loadbalancer (Resource)

HttpLoadbalancer resource

## Example Usage

```terraform
resource "f5xc_http_loadbalancer" "example" {
  name      = "example_http_loadbalancer"
  namespace = "example_namespace"
  waf_choice = {
    disable_waf = true
  }
  threat_mesh_choice = {
    enable_threat_mesh = true
  }
  advertise_choice = {
    advertise_on_public_default_vip = true
  }
  hash_policy_choice = {
    ring_hash = {
      ring_hash = "example_ring_hash"
    }
  }
  malicious_user_detection_choice = {
    enable_malicious_user_detection = true
  }
  trust_client_ip_headers_choice = {
    enable_trust_client_ip_headers = {
      enable_trust_client_ip_headers = "example_enable_trust_client_ip_headers"
    }
  }
  user_id_choice = {
    user_id_client_ip = true
  }
  api_discovery_choice = {
    enable_api_discovery = {
      enable_api_discovery = "example_enable_api_discovery"
    }
  }
  sensitive_data_policy_choice = {
    default_sensitive_data_policy = true
  }
  api_testing_choice = {
    disable_api_testing = true
  }
  loadbalancer_type_choice = {
    https = {
      https = "example_https"
    }
  }
  rate_limit_choice = {
    api_rate_limit = {
      api_rate_limit = "example_api_rate_limit"
    }
  }
  service_policy_choice = {
    service_policies_from_namespace = true
  }
  api_definition_choice = {
    disable_api_definition = true
  }
  domains = ["example_http_loadbalancer"]
  challenge_type_choice = {
    captcha_challenge = {
      captcha_challenge = "example_captcha_challenge"
    }
  }
  malware_protection_choice = {
    malware_protection_settings = {
      malware_protection_settings = "example_malware_protection_settings"
    }
  }
}
```

<!-- schema generated by tfplugindocs -->
## Schema

### Required

- `advertise_choice` (Attributes) Advertise choice VIP Advertisement choice, Internet or Custom. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--advertise_choice))
- `api_definition_choice` (Attributes) API Definition choice API Definition based on the imported swagger specs (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice))
- `api_discovery_choice` (Attributes) API Discovery Choice When enabled, sampled traffic passing through this load balancer is analyzed and APIs exposed by the application are identified. An API is identified by its canonical (collapsed) URL path and the method used to call it. Once all APIs are identified, for each API, the system learns its possible schema. The learnt schema for all APIs is used to automatically generate a swagger definition file for the entire API set exposed by this load balancer. The feature can be configured also via external app_type and labeling this HTTP Load Balancer. In this case external app_type configuration overrides internally disabled feature. Enabling the feature internally is allowed only when no labeling to external app_type. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_discovery_choice))
- `api_testing_choice` (Attributes) Api testing Enable or disable security testing for API endpoints to ensure a secure development pipeline. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_testing_choice))
- `challenge_type_choice` (Attributes) Challenge Type Malicious User Mitigation is an adaptive response and risk-based challenge capability. Enabling this feature would automatically serve different challenges such as JavaScript challenge, Captcha challenge or block temporarily based on the threat level of the malicious user. Policy based challenge can be used to selectively enable or disable JavaScript or Captcha challenge on a specific client and request match criteria (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice))
- `domains` (List of String) Domains A list of Domains (host/authority header) that will be matched to load balancer. Supported Domains and search order: 1. Exact Domain names: www.foo.com. 2. Domains starting with a Wildcard: *.foo.com. Not supported Domains: - Just a Wildcard: * - A Wildcard and TLD with no root Domain: *.com. - A Wildcard not matching a whole DNS label. e.g. *.foo.com and *.bar.foo.com are valid Wildcards however *bar.foo.com, *-bar.foo.com, and bar*.foo.com are all invalid. Additional notes: A Wildcard will not match empty string. e.g. *.foo.com will match bar.foo.com and baz-bar.foo.com but not .foo.com. The longest Wildcards match first. Only a single virtual host in the entire route configuration can match on *. Also a Domain must be unique across all virtual hosts within an advertise policy. Domains are also used for SNI matching if the Loadbalancer type is HTTPS. Domains also indicate the list of names for which DNS resolution will be automatically resolved to IP addresses by the system.
- `hash_policy_choice` (Attributes) Load Balancing Control The algorithm used to load balance requests between origin servers (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice))
- `loadbalancer_type_choice` (Attributes) Proxy choice The type of load balancer, can be 'http' or 'https' (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice))
- `malicious_user_detection_choice` (Attributes) Malicious User Detection Choice Malicious User Detection performs user behavior analysis and assigns a risk score and threat level of low, medium or high based on the suspicious activity of the user. The risk score of the user is decayed over time, if no further suspicious activity is noticed. The feature can be configured also via external app_type and labeling this HTTP Load Balancer. In this case external app_type configuration overrides internally disabled feature. Enabling the feature internally is allowed only when no labeling to external app_type. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--malicious_user_detection_choice))
- `malware_protection_choice` (Attributes) Malware Protection Malware Protection protects Web Apps and APIs, from malicious file uploads by scanning files in real-time. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--malware_protection_choice))
- `name` (String) name This is the name of configuration object. It has to be unique within the namespace. It can only be specified during create API and cannot be changed during replace API. The value of name has to follow DNS-1035 format.
- `namespace` (String) namespace This defines the workspace within which each the configuration object is to be created. Must be a DNS_LABEL format. For a namespace object itself, namespace value will be ''
- `rate_limit_choice` (Attributes) Enable Rate Limiting Rate Limiting allows you to control the rate of requests sent to your origin servers, protect against API traffic surge and denial of services attacks. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice))
- `sensitive_data_policy_choice` (Attributes) Sensitive Data Discovery Sensitive data discovery provide the ability to configure the policy for sensitive data detection (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--sensitive_data_policy_choice))
- `service_policy_choice` (Attributes) Service Policies Service policies provide the ability to configure security controls such as IP Allow/Deny lists, Geo IP filtering and Custom rules (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--service_policy_choice))
- `threat_mesh_choice` (Attributes) threat_mesh Threat Mesh provides F5 Distributed Cloud customers with an additional layer of protection against web application attacks. Threat Mesh leverages cross-customer correlation (i.e. correlation of client attacks across different customers) to identify malicious intent of a client. Whenever a client is flagged due to a malicious intent by our WAAP decisions engines, that client will be added to the ThreatDB. Enabling `Threat Mesh` means that requests that come to an application from an IP that is in the ThreatDB, will be automatically blocked by the load balancer. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--threat_mesh_choice))
- `trust_client_ip_headers_choice` (Attributes) Trusted Client IP Headers The system uses the real client IP address that initiated the connection to the platform as source IP, instead of the proxy’s IP address, when this feature is enabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--trust_client_ip_headers_choice))
- `user_id_choice` (Attributes) User Identifier Select user identifier for rate limiting and malicious user mitigation. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--user_id_choice))
- `waf_choice` (Attributes) Enable the WAF (Web Application Firewall) functionality for VirtualHost F5 Distributed Cloud WAF is a next-gen web application firewall that provides signature-based and behavior-based threat detection to protect applications wherever they are deployed. WAF can be configured in Monitoring (log threats) or Blocking (log and block threats) mode. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_choice))

### Optional

- `add_location` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `true`
- `annotations` (Map of String) annotations Annotations is an unstructured key value map stored with a resource that may be set by external tools to store and retrieve arbitrary metadata. They are not queryable and should be preserved when modifying objects.
- `blocked_clients` (Attributes List) Client Blocking Rules Define rules to block IP Prefixes or AS numbers. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--blocked_clients))
- `bot_defense_choice` (Attributes) Enable Bot Defense functionality for VirtualHost Bot Defense protects your applications from automated attacks by leveraging JavaScript and API calls to collect telemetry and mitigate sophisticated attacks within the context of F5's Distributed Cloud global network. When automation is detected, and mitigation action is set to Block or Redirect, the client will receive the configured mitigation response. If action is set to Flag, automated requests are monitored by Bot Defense, but are allowed to proceed to the customer's server. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice))
- `cache_options_choice` (Attributes) Enable CDN This feature belongs to the Content Delivery Network service. Enable it to enhance Console capabilities and utilize this feature. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--cache_options_choice))
- `client_side_defense_choice` (Attributes) Enable Client-Side Defense functionality for VirtualHost Client-Side Defense (CSD) provides a multi-phase protection system that protects web applications against Magecart, formjacking, digital skimming, PII harvesting and other malicious JavaScript attacks. Enabling CSD is a 2-part process (1) Service Enablement (2) JavaScript injection using a Load Balancer. By clicking enable in this section and going through the configuration, you can complete part 2 of the process. To enable the service itself, please visit the Service's Landing page using the search on the left navigation pane. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--client_side_defense_choice))
- `data_guard_rules` (Attributes List) Data Guard Rules Data Guard prevents responses from exposing sensitive information by masking the data. The system masks credit card numbers and social security numbers leaked from the application from within the http response with a string of asterisks (*). Note: App Firewall should be enabled, to use Data Guard feature. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--data_guard_rules))
- `ddos_mitigation_rules` (Attributes List) DDoS Mitigation Rules Define manual mitigation rules to block L7 DDoS attacks. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--ddos_mitigation_rules))
- `default_route_pools` (Attributes List) Default Route Origin Pools Origin Pools used when no route is specified (default route) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--default_route_pools))
- `description` (String) description Human readable description for the object
- `graphql_rules` (Attributes List) GraphQL Inspection GraphQL is a query language and server-side runtime for APIs which provides a complete and understandable description of the data in API. GraphQL gives clients the power to ask for exactly what they need, makes it easier to evolve APIs over time, and enables powerful developer tools. Policy configuration to analyze GraphQL queries and prevent GraphQL tailored attacks. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--graphql_rules))
- `host_name` (String) host name Internally generated host name to be used for the virtual host
- `host_rewrite_params_choice` (Attributes) HostRewriteParams Select how the host header can be modified during forwarding to the default origin servers(default route) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--host_rewrite_params_choice))
- `internet_vip_info` (Attributes List) Internet VIP Info Internet VIP Info (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--internet_vip_info))
- `ip_reputation_choice` (Attributes) ip_reputation IP reputation service analyzes IP threats and publishes a dynamic data set of millions of high-risk IP addresses, to protect users from inbound traffic from malicious IPs. IP threat categories include Spam Sources, Windows Exploits, Web Attacks, BotNets, Scanners, Denial of Service, Reputation, Phishing, Proxy, Mobile Threats, and TOR Proxy. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--ip_reputation_choice))
- `labels` (Map of String) labels Map of string keys and values that can be used to organize and categorize (scope and select) objects as chosen by the user. Values specified here will be used by selector expression
- `protected_cookies` (Attributes List) Cookie protection Allows setting attributes (SameSite, Secure, and HttpOnly) on cookies in responses. Cookie Tampering Protection prevents attackers from modifying the value of session cookies. For Cookie Tampering Protection, enabling a web app firewall (WAF) is a prerequisite. The configured mode of WAF (monitoring or blocking) will be enforced on the request when cookie tampering is identified. Note: We recommend enabling Secure and HttpOnly attributes along with cookie tampering protection. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--protected_cookies))
- `routes` (Attributes List) Routes Routes allow users to define match condition on a path and/or HTTP method to either forward matching traffic to origin pool or redirect matching traffic to a different URL or respond directly to matching traffic (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--routes))
- `slow_ddos_mitigation_choice` (Attributes) Slow DDoS Mitigation 'Slow and low' attacks tie up server resources, leaving none available for servicing requests from actual users.Configuration that protects origins from slow and low attacks. In the case HTTP loadbalancer, only default settings can be applied. For HTTPS load balancer, the request timeout and request headers timeout value can be customized. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--slow_ddos_mitigation_choice))
- `timeouts` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--timeouts))
- `trusted_clients` (Attributes List) Trusted Client Rules Define rules to skip processing of one or more features such as WAF, Bot Defense etc. for clients. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--trusted_clients))

### Read-Only

- `api_protection_rules` (Attributes) API Protection rules API Protection Rules can be defined in two categories. The first category includes fine-grained rules, per API path and methods. The second category includes rules per API groups or Server URLs. If request matches any rule in the first category, second category rules are not evaluated. Rules can also include additional conditions, for example specific clients can access certain API endpoint or API group. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_protection_rules))
- `auto_cert_info` (Attributes) Auto Cert Information Auto certificate related information (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--auto_cert_info))
- `cert_state` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["AutoCertError", "AutoCertDisabled", "AutoCertFinalize", "CertificateInvalid", "AutoCertNotApplicable", "AutoCertGenerationRetry", "PreDomainChallengePending", "DomainChallengeStarted", "DomainChallengePending", "DnsDomainVerification", "DomainChallengeVerified", "CertificateValid", "AutoCertAccountRateLimited", "AutoCertStarted", "AutoCertRateLimited", "AutoCertInitialize", "AutoCertDomainRateLimited", "CertificateExpired"]`
- `cors_policy` (Attributes) Cross-Origin Resource Sharing (CORS) configuration for requests CORS is a mechanism that uses additional HTTP headers to tell a browser to let a web application running at one origin (domain) have permission to access selected resources from a server at a different origin (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--cors_policy))
- `csrf_policy` (Attributes) csrf_policy Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF) is an attack that occurs when a malicious third-party website exploits a vulnerability thats allows them to submit an undesired request on user's behalf. The policy checks where a request is coming from to determine if the request's origin is the same as its destination. The policy relies on two pieces of information used in determining if a request originated from the same host. 1. The origin that caused the user agent to issue the request (source origin). 2. The origin that the request is going to (target origin). When the policy evaluating a request, it ensures both pieces of information are present and compare their values. If the source origin is missing or origins do not match the request is rejected. The exception to this being the source-origin has been added to the policy as valid. Because CSRF attacks specifically target state-changing requests, the policy only acts on the HTTP requests that have state-changing method (PUT,POST, etc.). (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--csrf_policy))
- `jwt_validation` (Attributes) JWT Validation JWT Validation stops JWT replay attacks and JWT tampering by cryptographically verifying incoming JWTs before they are passed to origin APIs. JWT Validation will also stop requests with expired tokens or tokens that are not yet valid. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation))
- `l7_ddos_protection` (Attributes) L7 DDoS Protection Settings L7 DDoS protection is critical for safeguarding web applications, APIs, and services that are exposed to the internet from sophisticated, volumetric, application-level threats. Configure actions, thresholds and policies to apply during L7 DDoS attack (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection))
- `more_option` (Attributes) More Options More options like header manipulation, compression etc. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option))
- `origin_server_subset_rule_list` (Attributes) Origin Server Subset Rules Origin Server Subset Rules allow users to define match condition on Client (IP address, ASN, Country), IP Reputation, Regional Edge names, Request for subset selection of origin servers. Origin Server Subset  is a sequential engine where rules are evaluated one after the other. It's important to define the correct order for Origin Server Subset  to get the intended result, rules are evaluated from top to bottom in the list. When an Origin server subset rule is matched, then this selection rule takes effect and no more rules are evaluated. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list))
- `sensitive_data_disclosure_rules` (Attributes) Sensitive Data Exposure Rules Sensitive Data Exposure Rules allows specifying rules to mask sensitive data fields in API responses (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules))
- `uid` (String) uid uid is the unique in time and space value for this object. Object create will fail if provided by the client and the value exists in the system. Typically generated by the server on successful creation of an object and is not allowed to change once populated. Shadowed by SystemObjectMeta's uid field.
- `waf_exclusion` (Attributes) WAF Exclusion Define the Signatures, Violations, Attack Types, and Bot Names that should be excluded from WAF processing for HTTP requests matching specific domain, path, and method criteria. The WAF Exclusion is evaluated sequentially and can only be matched once per request. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion))

Any other api-endpoint not listed will act according to 'Fall Through Mode' (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice))

### Nested Schema for `api_definition_choice.api_specification.validation_target_choice`

Optional:

- `validation_all_spec_endpoints` (Attributes) validation_all_spec_endpoints Validate all API endpoints specified by the API Inventory. All other API endpoints would proceed according to 'Fall Through Mode' (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints))
- `validation_custom_list` (Attributes) Custom list Define API groups, base paths, or API endpoints and their OpenAPI validation modes. Any other end-points not listed will act according to 'Fall Through Mode' (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_custom_list))
- `validation_disabled` (Boolean) Disabled Don't run OpenAPI validation

### Nested Schema for `api_definition_choice.api_specification.validation_target_choice.validation_all_spec_endpoints`

Required:

- `fall_through_mode` (Attributes) Fall Through Mode Determine what to do with unprotected endpoints (not part of the API Inventory or doesn't have a specific rule in custom rules) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode))
- `validation_mode` (Attributes) Validation Mode Validation mode of OpenAPI specification. When a validation mismatch occurs on a request to one of the API Inventory endpoints (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--validation_mode))

Optional:

- `settings` (Attributes) OpenAPI specification validation common settings OpenAPI specification validation settings relevant for 'API Inventory' enforcement and for 'Custom list' enforcement (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--settings))

<a id="nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode"></a>

Required:

- `fall_through_mode_choice` (Attributes) Fall Through Mode Determine what to do with unprotected endpoints (not in the OpenAPI specification file (a.k.a. swagger) or doesn't have a specific rule in custom rules) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice"></a>

Optional:

- `fall_through_mode_allow` (Boolean) Allow Allow any unprotected end point
- `fall_through_mode_custom` (Attributes) Custom Custom rules for any unprotected end point (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice--fall_through_mode_custom))

<a id="nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice--fall_through_mode_custom--open_api_validation_rules"></a>
### Nested Schema for `api_definition_choice.api_specification.validation_target_choice.validation_all_spec_endpoints.fall_through_mode.fall_through_mode_choice.fall_through_mode_custom.open_api_validation_rules`

Required:

- `action_choice` (Attributes) action choices Choose what action to do when the rule will apply (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice--fall_through_mode_custom--open_api_validation_rules--action_choice))

- `metadata` (Attributes) metadata Common attributes for the rule including name and description. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice--fall_through_mode_custom--open_api_validation_rules--metadata))

<a id="nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice--fall_through_mode_custom--open_api_validation_rules--action_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `api_definition_choice.api_specification.validation_target_choice.validation_all_spec_endpoints.fall_through_mode.fall_through_mode_choice.fall_through_mode_custom.open_api_validation_rules.action_choice`

Optional:

- `action_block` (Boolean) block Block the request and issue an API security event
- `action_report` (Boolean) report Continue processing the request and issue an API security event
- `action_skip` (Boolean) skip Continue processing the request


<a id="nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice--fall_through_mode_custom--open_api_validation_rules--condition_type_choice"></a>

Optional:

- `api_endpoint` (Attributes) API Endpoint The API endpoint (Path + Method) which this validation applies to (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--fall_through_mode--fall_through_mode_choice--fall_through_mode_custom--open_api_validation_rules--condition_type_choice--api_endpoint))
- `api_group` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(128)`
- `base_path` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(128)`

### Nested Schema for `api_definition_choice.api_specification.validation_target_choice.validation_all_spec_endpoints.validation_mode`

Required:

- `response_validation_mode_choice` (Attributes) Response Validation Mode Choice Validation mode of OpenAPI specification response. Indicates if enforcement is needed when a validation mismatch occurs on a response to one of the endpoints listed on the OpenAPI specification file (a.k.a. swagger) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--validation_mode--response_validation_mode_choice))
- `validation_mode_choice` (Attributes) Request Validation Mode Choice Validation mode of OpenAPI specification request. Indicates if enforcement is needed when a validation mismatch occurs on a request to one of the endpoints listed on the OpenAPI specification file (a.k.a. swagger) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--validation_mode--validation_mode_choice))

Optional:

- `skip_response_validation` (Boolean) Skip Skip OpenAPI validation processing for this event

<a id="nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--validation_mode--response_validation_mode_choice--response_validation_mode_active"></a>

Required:

Optional:

- `response_validation_properties` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["PROPERTY_PATH_PARAMETERS", "PROPERTY_CONTENT_TYPE", "PROPERTY_COOKIE_PARAMETERS", "PROPERTY_HTTP_HEADERS", "PROPERTY_HTTP_BODY", "PROPERTY_SECURITY_SCHEMA", "PROPERTY_RESPONSE_CODE", "PROPERTY_QUERY_PARAMETERS"]` (Multiple selection allowed)<br>**Default:** `PROPERTY_HTTP_HEADERS`

<a id="nestedatt--api_definition_choice--api_specification--validation_target_choice--validation_all_spec_endpoints--validation_mode--response_validation_mode_choice--response_validation_mode_active--validation_enforcement_type_choice"></a>

- `enforcement_report` (Boolean) Report Allow the response, trigger an API security event

- `disable_api_crawler` (Boolean) Crawler Disable Condition Select to turn off the API Crawling. No API Crawling actions will be performed.

You can only include domains you own that are associated with this Load Balancer. ### Nested Schema for `api_discovery_choice.enable_api_discovery.api_crawler.api_crawler_choice.api_crawler_config.domains.simple_login.password.secret_info_choice.blindfold_secret_info`

Required:

- `location` (String) Location Location is the uri_ref. It could be in url format for string:/// Or it could be a path if the store provider is an http/https location

Optional:

- `decryption_provider` (String) Decryption Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the backend Secret Management service.
- `store_provider` (String) Store Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///


<a id="nestedatt--api_discovery_choice--enable_api_discovery--api_crawler--api_crawler_choice--api_crawler_config--domains--simple_login--password--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info"></a>

Required:

- `url` (String) URL URL of the secret. Currently supported URL schemes is string:///. For string:/// scheme, Secret needs to be encoded Base64 format. When asked for this secret, caller will get Secret bytes after Base64 decoding.

Optional:

- `provider` (String) Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice`

Optional:

- `captcha_challenge` (Attributes) Captcha Challenge Configure Captcha challenge on this load balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--captcha_challenge))
- `enable_challenge` (Attributes) Enable Configure auto mitigation i.e risk based challenges for malicious users (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--enable_challenge))
- `js_challenge` (Attributes) JavaScript Challenge Configure JavaScript challenge on this load balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--js_challenge))
- `no_challenge` (Boolean) Disable No challenge is enabled for this load balancer
- `policy_based_challenge` (Attributes) policy based challenge Specifies the settings for policy rule based challenge (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge))

Optional:

- `captcha_challenge_parameters_choice` (Attributes) Captcha challenge parameters choice Select Default or Custom Parameters (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--enable_challenge--captcha_challenge_parameters_choice))
- `js_challenge_parameters_choice` (Attributes) JavaScript challenge parameters choice Select Default or Custom Parameters (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--enable_challenge--js_challenge_parameters_choice))
- `malicious_user_mitigation_choice` (Attributes) Malicious user mitigation choice Settings that specify the actions to be taken when malicious users are determined to be at different threat levels. User's activity is monitored and continuously analyzed for malicious behavior. From this analysis, a threat-level is assigned to each user. The settings defined in malicious user mitigation specify what mitigation actions to take for user determined to be at different threat levels. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--enable_challenge--malicious_user_mitigation_choice))

- `default_captcha_challenge_parameters` (Boolean) default parameters Use default parameters

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge`

Required:

- `challenge_choice` (Attributes) challenge choice Selection of different types of challenge. Selecting javascript or captcha shallenge will enable it for all traffic served by this load balancer. Policy based challenge can be used to define policy rules to enable or bypass challenge. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--challenge_choice))

Optional:

- `captcha_challenge_parameters_choice` (Attributes) Captcha challenge parameters choice Select Default or Custom Parameters (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--captcha_challenge_parameters_choice))
- `js_challenge_parameters_choice` (Attributes) JavaScript challenge parameters choice Select Default or Custom Parameters (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--js_challenge_parameters_choice))
- `malicious_user_mitigation_choice` (Attributes) Malicious user mitigation choice Settings that specify the actions to be taken when malicious users are determined to be at different threat levels. User's activity is monitored and continuously analyzed for malicious behavior. From this analysis, a threat-level is assigned to each user. The settings defined in malicious user mitigation specify what mitigation actions to take for user determined to be at different threat levels. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--malicious_user_mitigation_choice))
- `rule_list` (Attributes) Challenge rule list list challenge rules to be used in policy based challenge (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list))

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--challenge_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.challenge_choice`

Optional:

- `always_enable_captcha_challenge` (Boolean) always enable captcha challenge Enable Captcha challenge for all requests. Challenge rules can be used to selectively disable Captcha challenge or enable JavaScript challenge for some requests.
- `always_enable_js_challenge` (Boolean) always enable JS challenge Enable JavaScript challenge for all requests. Challenge rules can be used to selectively disable JavaScript challenge or enable Captcha challenge for some requests.
- `no_challenge` (Boolean) no_challenge Disable JavaScript and Captcha challenge for all requests. Challenge rules can be used to selectively enable JavaScript or Captcha challenge for some requests.


<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--captcha_challenge_parameters_choice"></a>

### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list`

Optional:

- `rules` (Attributes List) Rules Rules that specify the match conditions and challenge type to be launched. When a challenge type is selected to be always enabled, these rules can be used to disable challenge or launch a different challenge for requests that match the specified conditions (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules))

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules`

Required:

- `metadata` (Attributes) metadata Common attributes for the rule including name and description. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--metadata))
- `spec` (Attributes) spec Specification for the rule including match predicates and actions. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec))

Required:

- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtLeast(1)`

Optional:

- `description` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`


<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules.spec`

Required:

- `challenge_action_choice` (Attributes) Select Action Type Select challenge action, enable or disable challenge When enabled, the challenge type selected in PolicyBasedChallenge will be launched when match conditions in this challenge rule are met When the challenge type selected in PolicyBasedChallenge is always enable JS/Captcha challenge, the challenge can be disabled for certain requests which match the predicates defined in this rule by setting the action to disable_challenge (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--challenge_action_choice))

Optional:

- `arg_matchers` (Attributes List) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--arg_matchers))
- `asn_choice` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--asn_choice))
- `body_matcher` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--body_matcher))
- `client_choice` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--client_choice))
- `cookie_matchers` (Attributes List) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--cookie_matchers))
- `domain_matcher` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--domain_matcher))
- `expiration_timestamp` (String)
- `headers` (Attributes List) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--headers))
- `http_method` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--http_method))
- `ip_choice` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--ip_choice))
- `path` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--path))
- `query_params` (Attributes List) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--query_params))
- `tls_fingerprint_choice` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--tls_fingerprint_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--challenge_action_choice"></a>

Optional:

- `disable_challenge` (Boolean) Disable challenge Disable the challenge type selected in PolicyBasedChallenge
- `enable_captcha_challenge` (Boolean) Enable captcha challenge Enable captcha challenge
- `enable_javascript_challenge` (Boolean) Enable javascript challenge Enable javascript challenge


<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--arg_matchers"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules.spec.arg_matchers`

Required:

- `match_choice` (Attributes) match Select Match Options. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--arg_matchers--match_choice))
- `name` (String) name A case-sensitive JSON path in the HTTP request body.

Optional:

- `invert_matcher` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--arg_matchers--match_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules.spec.arg_matchers.match_choice`

Optional:

- `check_not_present` (Boolean) check_not_present Check that the argument is not present.
- `check_present` (Boolean) check_present Check that the argument is present.
- `item` (Attributes) item Criteria for matching the values for the Arg. The match is successful if any of the values in the input satisfies the criteria in the matcher. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--arg_matchers--match_choice--item))

Optional:

- `exact_values` (List of String) exact values A list of exact values to match the input against.
- `regex_values` (List of String) regex values A list of regular expressions to match the input against.
- `transformers` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["TRANSFORMER_NONE", "UPPER_CASE", "BASE64_DECODE", "URL_DECODE", "TRIM_LEFT", "LOWER_CASE", "NORMALIZE_PATH", "REMOVE_WHITESPACE", "TRIM_RIGHT", "TRIM"]` (Multiple selection allowed)

### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules.spec.query_params`

Required:

- `key` (String) key A case-sensitive HTTP query parameter name.
- `match_choice` (Attributes) match Select Match Options. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--query_params--match_choice))

Optional:

- `invert_matcher` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--query_params--match_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules.spec.query_params.match_choice`

Optional:

- `check_not_present` (Boolean) check_not_present Check that the query parameter is not present.
- `check_present` (Boolean) check_present Check that the query parameter is present.
- `item` (Attributes) item Criteria for matching the values for the given query parameter. The match is successful if any of the values for the query parameter satisfies the criteria in the matcher. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--query_params--match_choice--item))

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--query_params--match_choice--item"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules.spec.query_params.match_choice.item`

Optional:

- `exact_values` (List of String) exact values A list of exact values to match the input against.
- `regex_values` (List of String) regex values A list of regular expressions to match the input against.
- `transformers` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["BASE64_DECODE", "URL_DECODE", "TRIM_LEFT", "LOWER_CASE", "NORMALIZE_PATH", "REMOVE_WHITESPACE", "TRIM_RIGHT", "TRIM", "TRANSFORMER_NONE", "UPPER_CASE"]` (Multiple selection allowed)




<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--tls_fingerprint_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules.spec.tls_fingerprint_choice`

Optional:

- `tls_fingerprint_matcher` (Attributes) (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--tls_fingerprint_choice--tls_fingerprint_matcher))

<a id="nestedatt--challenge_type_choice--policy_based_challenge--rule_list--rules--spec--tls_fingerprint_choice--tls_fingerprint_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `challenge_type_choice.policy_based_challenge.rule_list.rules.spec.tls_fingerprint_choice.tls_fingerprint_matcher`

Optional:

- `classes` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["TORRENTLOCKER", "TLS_FINGERPRINT_NONE", "ANY_MALICIOUS_FINGERPRINT", "DRIDEX", "RANSOMWARE", "TOFSEE", "TRICKBOT", "ADWARE", "ADWIND", "GOOTKIT", "GOZI", "JBIFROST", "QUAKBOT", "TROLDESH"]` (Multiple selection allowed)
- `exact_values` (List of String) exact values A list of exact TLS JA3 fingerprints to match the input TLS JA3 fingerprint against.
- `excluded_values` (List of String) excluded values A list of TLS JA3 fingerprints to be excluded when matching the input TLS JA3 fingerprint. This can be used to skip known false positives when using one or more known TLS fingerprint classes in the enclosing matcher.








<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice`

Optional:

- `cookie_stickiness` (Attributes) Cookie Based Stickiness Request are sent to all eligible origin servers using hash of cookie. Cookie can be passive(present in the cookies header) or generated(hashing the source and destination ports and addresses). Consistent hashing algorithm, ring hash, is used to select origin server (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--cookie_stickiness))
- `least_active` (Boolean) Least Active Requests Request are sent to origin server that has least active requests
- `random` (Boolean) Random Request are sent to all eligible origin servers in random fashion
- `ring_hash` (Attributes) Ring Hash Policy Request are sent to all eligible origin servers using hash of request based on hash policy. Consistent hashing algorithm, ring hash, is used to select origin server (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash))
- `round_robin` (Boolean) Round Robin Request are sent to all eligible origin servers in round robin fashion
- `source_ip_stickiness` (Boolean) Source IP Stickiness Request are sent to all eligible origin servers using hash of source ip. Consistent hashing algorithm, ring hash, is used to select origin server

<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--cookie_stickiness"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.cookie_stickiness`

Required:

- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 256)`

Optional:

- `httponly_choice` (Attributes) httponly A cookie with the HttpOnly attribute is inaccessible to the JavaScript. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--cookie_stickiness--httponly_choice))
- `path` (String) path The name of the path for the cookie. If no path is specified here, no path will be set for the cookie
- `samesite_choice` (Attributes) samesite Controls whether or not a cookie is sent with cross-site requests (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--cookie_stickiness--samesite_choice))
- `secure_choice` (Attributes) secure A cookie with the Secure attribute is only sent to the server with an encrypted request over the HTTPS protocol and therefore, is more resistant to man-in-the-middle attacks. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--cookie_stickiness--secure_choice))
- `ttl` (Number) ttl If specified, a cookie with the TTL will be generated if the cookie is not present. If the TTL is present and zero, the generated cookie will be a session cookie. TTL value is in milliseconds

<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--cookie_stickiness--httponly_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.cookie_stickiness.httponly_choice`

Optional:

- `add_httponly` (Boolean) add_httponly Add httponly attribute
- `ignore_httponly` (Boolean) ignore_httponly Ignore httponly attribute


<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--cookie_stickiness--samesite_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.cookie_stickiness.samesite_choice`

Optional:

- `ignore_samesite` (Boolean) ignore_samesite Ignore Samesite attribute
- `samesite_lax` (Boolean) lax Add Samesite attribute with Lax. Means that the cookie is not sent on cross-site requests
- `samesite_none` (Boolean) none Add Samesite attribute with None. Means that the browser sends the cookie with both cross-site and same-site requests
- `samesite_strict` (Boolean) strict Add Samesite attribute with Strict. Means that the browser sends the cookie only for same-site requests


<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--cookie_stickiness--secure_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.cookie_stickiness.secure_choice`

Optional:

- `add_secure` (Boolean) add_secure Add secure attribute
- `ignore_secure` (Boolean) ignore_secure Ignore secure attribute



<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.ring_hash`

Required:

- `hash_policy` (Attributes List) hash_policy Specifies a list of hash policies to use for ring hash load balancing. Each hash policy is evaluated individually and the combined result is used to route the request (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy))

<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.ring_hash.hash_policy`

Required:

- `policy_specifier_choice` (Attributes) PolicySpecifier Specifies whether hashing is done on incoming request header, cookie or source IP (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice))

Optional:

- `terminal` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.ring_hash.hash_policy.policy_specifier_choice`

Optional:

- `cookie` (Attributes) Cookie Hash based on cookie (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice--cookie))
- `header_name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 256)`
- `source_ip` (Boolean) Source IP Hash based on source IP address

<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice--cookie"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.ring_hash.hash_policy.policy_specifier_choice.cookie`

Required:

- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 256)`

Optional:

- `httponly_choice` (Attributes) httponly A cookie with the HttpOnly attribute is inaccessible to the JavaScript. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice--cookie--httponly_choice))
- `path` (String) path The name of the path for the cookie. If no path is specified here, no path will be set for the cookie
- `samesite_choice` (Attributes) samesite Controls whether or not a cookie is sent with cross-site requests (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice--cookie--samesite_choice))
- `secure_choice` (Attributes) secure A cookie with the Secure attribute is only sent to the server with an encrypted request over the HTTPS protocol and therefore, is more resistant to man-in-the-middle attacks. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice--cookie--secure_choice))
- `ttl` (Number) ttl If specified, a cookie with the TTL will be generated if the cookie is not present. If the TTL is present and zero, the generated cookie will be a session cookie. TTL value is in milliseconds

<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice--cookie--httponly_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.ring_hash.hash_policy.policy_specifier_choice.cookie.httponly_choice`

Optional:

- `add_httponly` (Boolean) add_httponly Add httponly attribute
- `ignore_httponly` (Boolean) ignore_httponly Ignore httponly attribute


<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice--cookie--samesite_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.ring_hash.hash_policy.policy_specifier_choice.cookie.samesite_choice`

Optional:

- `ignore_samesite` (Boolean) ignore_samesite Ignore Samesite attribute
- `samesite_lax` (Boolean) lax Add Samesite attribute with Lax. Means that the cookie is not sent on cross-site requests
- `samesite_none` (Boolean) none Add Samesite attribute with None. Means that the browser sends the cookie with both cross-site and same-site requests
- `samesite_strict` (Boolean) strict Add Samesite attribute with Strict. Means that the browser sends the cookie only for same-site requests


<a id="nestedatt--hash_policy_choice--ring_hash--hash_policy--policy_specifier_choice--cookie--secure_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `hash_policy_choice.ring_hash.hash_policy.policy_specifier_choice.cookie.secure_choice`

Optional:

- `add_secure` (Boolean) add_secure Add secure attribute
- `ignore_secure` (Boolean) ignore_secure Ignore secure attribute







<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice`

Optional:

- `http` (Attributes) HTTP HTTP Load Balancer. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--http))
- `https` (Attributes) HTTPS HTTPS load balancer with a custom public/private certificate. This is also known as BYOC (Bring Your Own Certificate). User is responsible for managing DNS to this load balancer. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https))
- `https_auto_cert` (Attributes) HTTPS HTTPS load balancer with automatic public certificate provisioning. DNS records for the domains will be automatically managed by F5 Distributed Cloud. As a prerequisite, the domain must be delegated to F5 Distributed Cloud (see the DNS Management section) or a DNS CNAME record should be created in your DNS provider's portal(only for Domains not managed by F5 Distributed Cloud). (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--http"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.http`

Required:

- `port_choice` (Attributes) HTTP Port Choice Available options: user provided port or system default port or port range (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--http--port_choice))

Optional:

- `dns_volterra_managed` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--http--port_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.http.port_choice`

Optional:

- `port` (Number) <br>**Default:** `80`
- `port_ranges` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 512)`



<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https`

Required:

- `path_normalize_choice` (Attributes) Normalize Path URI Path is normalized and adjacent slashes in the path are merged into one. The platform does not perform case normalization. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--path_normalize_choice))
- `port_choice` (Attributes) HTTPS Port Choice Available options: user provided port or system default port or port range (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--port_choice))
- `tls_certificates_choice` (Attributes) TLS Configuration TLS Certificate Configuration for downstream connections (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice))

Optional:

- `add_hsts` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `coalescing_options` (Attributes) TLS Coalescing options Options for coalescing TLS for multiple HTTPS Load Balancers (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--coalescing_options))
- `connection_idle_timeout` (Number) <br>**Default:** `120000`
- `default_lb_choice` (Attributes) Default Load Balancer Designate the Load balancer as default for clients that don't present the SNI header in TLS handshake. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--default_lb_choice))
- `http_protocol_options` (Attributes) http_protocol_options HTTP protocol configuration options for downstream connections. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options))
- `http_redirect` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `server_header_choice` (Attributes) Server Header configuration Define the settings of “server” response header. A response header with name “server” and value “volt-adc” is added to responses by default. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--server_header_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--path_normalize_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.path_normalize_choice`

Optional:

- `disable_path_normalize` (Boolean) Disable Path normalization
- `enable_path_normalize` (Boolean) Enable Path normalization


<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--port_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.port_choice`

Optional:

- `port` (Number) <br>**Default:** `443`
- `port_ranges` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 512)`


<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice`

Optional:

- `tls_cert_params` (Attributes) TLS Certificates Select/Add one or more TLS Certificate objects to associate with this Load Balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params))
- `tls_parameters` (Attributes) Inline TLS Parameters (legacy) Upload a TLS certificate covering all domain names for this Load Balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params`

Required:

- `certificates` (Attributes List) certificates Select one or more certificates with any domain names. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--certificates))
- `mtls_choice` (Attributes) mTLS choice mTLS authentication between clients and HTTPS load balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice))

Optional:

- `tls_config` (Attributes) TLS Config Configuration of TLS settings such as min/max TLS version and ciphersuites (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--tls_config))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--certificates"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.certificates`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)


<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.mtls_choice`

Optional:

- `no_mtls` (Boolean) No mTLS
- `use_mtls` (Attributes) Use mTLS (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.mtls_choice.use_mtls`

Required:

- `trusted_ca_choice` (Attributes) trusted_ca_choice Root CA configuration for downstream TLS connections (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--trusted_ca_choice))

Optional:

- `client_certificate_optional` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `crl_choice` (Attributes) crl choice Whether to verify client certificate revocation status with certificate revocation list (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--crl_choice))
- `xfcc_header_choice` (Attributes) xfcc header Whether to add the XFCC header with information about the client certificate (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--xfcc_header_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--trusted_ca_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.mtls_choice.use_mtls.trusted_ca_choice`

Optional:

- `trusted_ca` (Attributes) trusted_ca Select/Add a Root CA Certificate object to associate with this Load Balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--trusted_ca_choice--trusted_ca))
- `trusted_ca_url` (String) trusted_ca_url Upload a Root CA Certificate specifically for this Load Balancer

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--trusted_ca_choice--trusted_ca"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.mtls_choice.use_mtls.trusted_ca_choice.trusted_ca`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)



<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--crl_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.mtls_choice.use_mtls.crl_choice`

Optional:

- `crl` (Attributes) Verify client certificate with CRL Client certificate is verified against CRL. Specify the CRL server information to download the certificate revocation list (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--crl_choice--crl))
- `no_crl` (Boolean) No client certificate verification with CRL Client certificate revocation status is not verified

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--crl_choice--crl"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.mtls_choice.use_mtls.crl_choice.crl`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)



<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--xfcc_header_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.mtls_choice.use_mtls.xfcc_header_choice`

Optional:

- `xfcc_disabled` (Boolean) No XFCC header No X-Forwarded-Client-Cert header will be added
- `xfcc_options` (Attributes) Add XFCC header X-Forwarded-Client-Cert header will be added with the configured fields (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--mtls_choice--use_mtls--xfcc_header_choice--xfcc_options))

Required:

- `xfcc_header_elements` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["XFCC_NONE", "XFCC_CERT", "XFCC_CHAIN", "XFCC_SUBJECT", "XFCC_URI", "XFCC_DNS"]` (Multiple selection allowed)





<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--tls_config"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.tls_config`

Required:

- `choice` (Attributes) TLS Security Level The security level for TLS handshake between client/server and the platform. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--tls_config--choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--tls_config--choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.tls_config.choice`

Optional:

- `custom_security` (Attributes) Custom Security Custom selection of TLS versions and cipher suites (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--tls_config--choice--custom_security))
- `default_security` (Boolean) Default Security TLS v1.2+ with PFS ciphers and strong crypto algorithms.
- `low_security` (Boolean) Low Security TLS v1.0+ including non-PFS ciphers and weak crypto algorithms.
- `medium_security` (Boolean) Medium Security TLS v1.0+ with PFS ciphers and medium strength crypto algorithms.

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_cert_params--tls_config--choice--custom_security"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_cert_params.tls_config.choice.custom_security`

Required:

- `cipher_suites` (List of String) cipher_suites The TLS listener will only support the specified cipher list.

Optional:

- `max_version` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["TLS_AUTO", "TLSv1_0", "TLSv1_1", "TLSv1_2", "TLSv1_3"]`
- `min_version` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["TLSv1_2", "TLSv1_3", "TLS_AUTO", "TLSv1_0", "TLSv1_1"]`





<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters`

- `mtls_choice` (Attributes) mTLS choice mTLS authentication between clients and HTTPS load balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice))
- `tls_certificates` (Attributes List) tls_certificates Users can add one or more certificates that share the same set of domains. for example, domain.com and *.domain.com - but use different signature algorithms (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates))

Optional:

- `tls_config` (Attributes) TLS Config Configuration of TLS settings such as min/max TLS version and ciphersuites (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_config))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.mtls_choice`

Optional:

- `no_mtls` (Boolean) No mTLS
- `use_mtls` (Attributes) Use mTLS (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.mtls_choice.use_mtls`

Required:

- `trusted_ca_choice` (Attributes) trusted_ca_choice Root CA configuration for downstream TLS connections (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--trusted_ca_choice))

Optional:

- `client_certificate_optional` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `crl_choice` (Attributes) crl choice Whether to verify client certificate revocation status with certificate revocation list (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--crl_choice))
- `xfcc_header_choice` (Attributes) xfcc header Whether to add the XFCC header with information about the client certificate (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--xfcc_header_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--trusted_ca_choice"></a>

Optional:

- `trusted_ca` (Attributes) trusted_ca Select/Add a Root CA Certificate object to associate with this Load Balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--trusted_ca_choice--trusted_ca))
- `trusted_ca_url` (String) trusted_ca_url Upload a Root CA Certificate specifically for this Load Balancer

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

Specify the CRL server information to download the certificate revocation list (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--crl_choice--crl))
- `no_crl` (Boolean) No client certificate verification with CRL Client certificate revocation status is not verified

Read-Only:
