<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--xfcc_header_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.mtls_choice.use_mtls.xfcc_header_choice`

Optional:

- `xfcc_disabled` (Boolean) No XFCC header No X-Forwarded-Client-Cert header will be added
- `xfcc_options` (Attributes) Add XFCC header X-Forwarded-Client-Cert header will be added with the configured fields (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--xfcc_header_choice--xfcc_options))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--mtls_choice--use_mtls--xfcc_header_choice--xfcc_options"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.mtls_choice.use_mtls.xfcc_header_choice.xfcc_options`

Required:

- `xfcc_header_elements` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["XFCC_CHAIN", "XFCC_SUBJECT", "XFCC_URI", "XFCC_DNS", "XFCC_NONE", "XFCC_CERT"]` (Multiple selection allowed)





<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_certificates`

Required:

- `certificate_url` (String) certificate_url TLS certificate. Certificate or certificate chain in PEM format including the PEM headers.
- `private_key` (Attributes) private_key TLS Private Key data in unencrypted PEM format including the PEM headers. The data may be optionally secured using BlindFold. TLS key has to match the accompanying certificate. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--private_key))

Optional:

- `description` (String) description Description for the certificate
- `ocsp_stapling_choice` (Attributes) OCSP Response choice OCSP Stapling choice (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--ocsp_stapling_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--private_key"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_certificates.private_key`

Required:

- `secret_info_choice` (Attributes) SecretInfoOneof Secret can be one of the following types - * Blindfold Secret - Secret is managed by F5XC Secret Management System * Clear Secret - Secret is not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--private_key--secret_info_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--private_key--secret_info_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_certificates.private_key.secret_info_choice`

Optional:

- `blindfold_secret_info` (Attributes) Blindfold Secret Blindfold Secret is used for the secrets managed by F5XC Secret Management Service (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--private_key--secret_info_choice--blindfold_secret_info))
- `clear_secret_info` (Attributes) Clear Secret Clear Secret is used for the secrets that are not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--private_key--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--private_key--secret_info_choice--blindfold_secret_info"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_certificates.private_key.secret_info_choice.blindfold_secret_info`

Required:

- `location` (String) Location Location is the uri_ref. It could be in url format for string:/// Or it could be a path if the store provider is an http/https location

Optional:

- `decryption_provider` (String) Decryption Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the backend Secret Management service.
- `store_provider` (String) Store Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///


<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--private_key--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_certificates.private_key.secret_info_choice.clear_secret_info`

Required:

- `url` (String) URL URL of the secret. Currently supported URL schemes is string:///. For string:/// scheme, Secret needs to be encoded Base64 format. When asked for this secret, caller will get Secret bytes after Base64 decoding.

Optional:

- `provider` (String) Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///




<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--ocsp_stapling_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_certificates.ocsp_stapling_choice`

Optional:

- `custom_hash_algorithms` (Attributes) Use Custom Order for Hash Algorithms Use hash algorithms in the custom order. F5XC will try to fetch ocsp response from the CA in the given order. Additionally, LoadBalancer will not become active until ocspResponse cannot be fetched if the certificate has MustStaple extension set. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--ocsp_stapling_choice--custom_hash_algorithms))
- `disable_ocsp_stapling` (Boolean) Disable OCSP Stapling Disable OCSP Stapling. F5XC will not fetch and staple OCSP Response for this certificate. This is the default behavior if no choice is selected.
- `use_system_defaults` (Boolean) Fetch with F5XC default settings Use F5XC Default Settings to fetch and staple OCSP Response. OCSP Response will be stapled if it can be fetched. Additionally, LoadBalancer will not become active until ocspResponse cannot be fetched if the certificate has MustStaple extension set. F5XC will try to fetch OCSPResponse with sha256 and sha1 as HashAlgorithm, in that order.

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_certificates--ocsp_stapling_choice--custom_hash_algorithms"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_certificates.ocsp_stapling_choice.custom_hash_algorithms`

Required:

- `hash_algorithms` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["INVALID_HASH_ALGORITHM", "SHA256", "SHA1"]` (Multiple selection allowed)




<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_config"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_config`

Required:

- `choice` (Attributes) TLS Security Level The security level for TLS handshake between client/server and the platform. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_config--choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_config--choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_config.choice`

Optional:

- `custom_security` (Attributes) Custom Security Custom selection of TLS versions and cipher suites (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_config--choice--custom_security))
- `default_security` (Boolean) Default Security TLS v1.2+ with PFS ciphers and strong crypto algorithms.
- `low_security` (Boolean) Low Security TLS v1.0+ including non-PFS ciphers and weak crypto algorithms.
- `medium_security` (Boolean) Medium Security TLS v1.0+ with PFS ciphers and medium strength crypto algorithms.

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--tls_certificates_choice--tls_parameters--tls_config--choice--custom_security"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.tls_certificates_choice.tls_parameters.tls_config.choice.custom_security`

Required:

- `cipher_suites` (List of String) cipher_suites The TLS listener will only support the specified cipher list.

Optional:

- `max_version` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["TLSv1_3", "TLS_AUTO", "TLSv1_0", "TLSv1_1", "TLSv1_2"]`
- `min_version` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["TLS_AUTO", "TLSv1_0", "TLSv1_1", "TLSv1_2", "TLSv1_3"]`






<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--coalescing_options"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.coalescing_options`

Required:

- `coalescing_choice` (Attributes) TLS Coalescing choice Coalescing of multiple HTTPS Loadbalancers into single one (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--coalescing_options--coalescing_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--coalescing_options--coalescing_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.coalescing_options.coalescing_choice`

Optional:

- `default_coalescing` (Boolean) Default Coalescing HTTPS Loadbalancers are coalesced even when they have different TLS version or Cipher suite configuration
- `strict_coalescing` (Boolean) Strict Coalescing HTTPS Loadbalancers are not coalesced when they have different TLS version and/or Cipher suite configuration



<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--default_lb_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.default_lb_choice`

Optional:

- `default_loadbalancer` (Boolean) Default load balancer
- `non_default_loadbalancer` (Boolean) Not a default load balancer


<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.http_protocol_options`

Required:

- `http_protocol_choice` (Attributes) HTTP Protocol Configuration HTTP protocol configuration options for downstream connections (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.http_protocol_options.http_protocol_choice`

Optional:

- `http_protocol_enable_v1_only` (Attributes) http_protocol_enable_v1_only Enable HTTP/1.1 for downstream connections (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only))
- `http_protocol_enable_v1_v2` (Boolean) http_protocol_enable_v1_v2 Enable both HTTP/1.1 and HTTP/2 for downstream connections
- `http_protocol_enable_v2_only` (Boolean) http_protocol_enable_v2_only Enable HTTP/2 for downstream connections

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.http_protocol_options.http_protocol_choice.http_protocol_enable_v1_only`

Optional:

- `header_transformation` (Attributes) Header transformation Two mutually exclusive types of header key formatters are supported: Stateless (Default and Proper Case) and Stateful (Preserve Case) formatters. When a Stateless formatter is selected, it applies to response headers sent to the client and when a Stateful formatter is selected, it applies to the upstream request headers. It's essential to ensure that the same formatter type (either stateless or stateful) is applied on origin pool. If different formatter types are applied, only the stateful formatter will take effect, and the stateless formatter will be disregarded. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only--header_transformation))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only--header_transformation"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.http_protocol_options.http_protocol_choice.http_protocol_enable_v1_only.header_transformation`

Required:

- `header_transformation_choice` (Attributes) Header transformation Select header transformation options for HTTP/1.1 (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only--header_transformation--header_transformation_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only--header_transformation--header_transformation_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.http_protocol_options.http_protocol_choice.http_protocol_enable_v1_only.header_transformation.header_transformation_choice`

Optional:

- `default_header_transformation` (Boolean) Default header transformation Normalize the headers to lower case
- `legacy_header_transformation` (Boolean) Legacy header transformations Use old header transformation if configured earlier
- `preserve_case_header_transformation` (Boolean) Preserve case header transformation Preserves the original case of headers without any modifications.
- `proper_case_header_transformation` (Boolean) Proper case header transformation Normalize the headers to proper case words. The fist character and any character following a special character will be capitalized if it’s an alpha character. For example, “content-type” becomes “Content-Type”, and “foo$b#$are” becomes “Foo$B#$Are”






<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https--server_header_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https.server_header_choice`

Optional:

- `append_server_name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(8096)`
- `default_header` (Boolean) default_header Response header name is “server” and value is “volt-adc”
- `pass_through` (Boolean) pass_through Pass existing server header as is. If server header is absent, a new header is not appended.
- `server_name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(8096)`



<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert`

Required:

- `mtls_choice` (Attributes) mTLS choice mTLS authentication between clients and HTTPS load balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--mtls_choice))
- `path_normalize_choice` (Attributes) Normalize Path URI Path is normalized and adjacent slashes in the path are merged into one. The platform does not perform case normalization. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--path_normalize_choice))

Optional:

- `add_hsts` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `coalescing_options` (Attributes) TLS Coalescing options Options for coalescing TLS for multiple HTTPS Load Balancers (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--coalescing_options))
- `connection_idle_timeout` (Number) <br>**Default:** `120000`
- `default_lb_choice` (Attributes) Default Load Balancer For clients that don't specify the SNI field in the TLS handshake, designate this Load Balancer as default among any listening on the same VIP and port. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--default_lb_choice))
- `http_protocol_options` (Attributes) http_protocol_options HTTP protocol configuration options for downstream connections. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--http_protocol_options))
- `http_redirect` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `port_choice` (Attributes) HTTPS Port Choice Available options: user provided port or system default port or port range (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--port_choice))
- `server_header_choice` (Attributes) Server Header configuration Define the settings of “server” response header. A response header with name “server” and value “volt-adc” is added to responses by default. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--server_header_choice))
- `tls_config` (Attributes) TLS Config Configuration of TLS settings such as min/max TLS version and ciphersuites (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--tls_config))

- `no_mtls` (Boolean) No mTLS
- `use_mtls` (Attributes) Use mTLS (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--mtls_choice--use_mtls))

- `http_protocol_enable_v1_v2` (Boolean) http_protocol_enable_v1_v2 Enable both HTTP/1.1 and HTTP/2 for downstream connections
- `http_protocol_enable_v2_only` (Boolean) http_protocol_enable_v2_only Enable HTTP/2 for downstream connections

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert.http_protocol_options.http_protocol_choice.http_protocol_enable_v1_only`

Optional:

- `header_transformation` (Attributes) Header transformation Two mutually exclusive types of header key formatters are supported: Stateless (Default and Proper Case) and Stateful (Preserve Case) formatters. When a Stateless formatter is selected, it applies to response headers sent to the client and when a Stateful formatter is selected, it applies to the upstream request headers. It's essential to ensure that the same formatter type (either stateless or stateful) is applied on origin pool. If different formatter types are applied, only the stateful formatter will take effect, and the stateless formatter will be disregarded. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only--header_transformation))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only--header_transformation"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert.http_protocol_options.http_protocol_choice.http_protocol_enable_v1_only.header_transformation`

Required:

- `header_transformation_choice` (Attributes) Header transformation Select header transformation options for HTTP/1.1 (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only--header_transformation--header_transformation_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--http_protocol_options--http_protocol_choice--http_protocol_enable_v1_only--header_transformation--header_transformation_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert.http_protocol_options.http_protocol_choice.http_protocol_enable_v1_only.header_transformation.header_transformation_choice`

Optional:

- `default_header_transformation` (Boolean) Default header transformation Normalize the headers to lower case
- `legacy_header_transformation` (Boolean) Legacy header transformations Use old header transformation if configured earlier
- `preserve_case_header_transformation` (Boolean) Preserve case header transformation Preserves the original case of headers without any modifications.
- `proper_case_header_transformation` (Boolean) Proper case header transformation Normalize the headers to proper case words. The fist character and any character following a special character will be capitalized if it’s an alpha character. For example, “content-type” becomes “Content-Type”, and “foo$b#$are” becomes “Foo$B#$Are”






<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--port_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert.port_choice`

Optional:

- `port` (Number) <br>**Default:** `443`
- `port_ranges` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 512)`


<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--server_header_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert.server_header_choice`

Optional:

- `append_server_name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(8096)`
- `default_header` (Boolean) default_header Response header name is “server” and value is “volt-adc”
- `pass_through` (Boolean) pass_through Pass existing server header as is. If server header is absent, a new header is not appended.
- `server_name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(8096)`


<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--tls_config"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert.tls_config`

Required:

- `choice` (Attributes) TLS Security Level The security level for TLS handshake between client/server and the platform. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--tls_config--choice))

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--tls_config--choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert.tls_config.choice`

Optional:

- `custom_security` (Attributes) Custom Security Custom selection of TLS versions and cipher suites (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--tls_config--choice--custom_security))
- `default_security` (Boolean) Default Security TLS v1.2+ with PFS ciphers and strong crypto algorithms.
- `low_security` (Boolean) Low Security TLS v1.0+ including non-PFS ciphers and weak crypto algorithms.
- `medium_security` (Boolean) Medium Security TLS v1.0+ with PFS ciphers and medium strength crypto algorithms.

<a id="nestedatt--loadbalancer_type_choice--https_auto_cert--tls_config--choice--custom_security"></a>
### Nested Schema for `loadbalancer_type_choice.https_auto_cert.tls_config.choice.custom_security`

Required:

- `cipher_suites` (List of String) cipher_suites The TLS listener will only support the specified cipher list.

Optional:

- `max_version` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["TLSv1_1", "TLSv1_2", "TLSv1_3", "TLS_AUTO", "TLSv1_0"]`
- `min_version` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["TLS_AUTO", "TLSv1_0", "TLSv1_1", "TLSv1_2", "TLSv1_3"]`

- `enable_malicious_user_detection` (Boolean) Enable malicious user detection

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice`

Optional:

- `api_rate_limit` (Attributes) Rate Limiting Parameters Define rate limiting for one or more API endpoints. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit))
- `disable_rate_limit` (Boolean) Disable Rate Limiting Rate limiting is not currently enabled for this load balancer
- `rate_limit` (Attributes) Custom Rate Limiting Parameters Define custom rate limiting parameters for this load balancer (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit`

Required:

- `ip_allowed_list_choice` (Attributes) IP Allowed List Allowed List of IPs for which rate limiting will be disabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice))

Optional:

- `api_endpoint_rules` (Attributes List) api_endpoint_policy Sets of rules for a specific endpoints. Order is matter as it uses first match policy. For creating rule that contain a whole domain or group of endpoints, please use the server URL rules above. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules))
- `server_url_rules` (Attributes List) server_url_policy Set of rules for entire domain or base path that contain multiple endpoints. Order is matter as it uses first match policy. For matching also specific endpoints you can use the API endpoint rules set bellow. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice`

Optional:

- `bypass_rate_limiting_rules` (Attributes) bypass_rate_limiting_policy This category defines rules per URL or API group. If request matches any of these rules, skip Rate Limiting. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules))
- `custom_ip_allowed_list` (Attributes) Custom IP list IP Allowed list using existing ip_prefix_set objects. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--custom_ip_allowed_list))
- `ip_allowed_list` (Attributes) IP Allowed List List of IP(s) for which rate limiting will be disabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--ip_allowed_list))
- `no_ip_allowed_list` (Boolean) No IP Allowed List There is no ip allowed list for rate limiting, all clients go through rate limiting.

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice.bypass_rate_limiting_rules`

Optional:

- `bypass_rate_limiting_rules` (Attributes List) bypass_rate_limiting_policy This category defines rules per URL or API group. If request matches any of these rules, skip Rate Limiting. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules"></a>

Required:

- `domain_choice` (Attributes) domain choices Choose if the rule will apply for all domains or a specific one. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--domain_choice))

Optional:

- `client_matcher` (Attributes) client_matcher Conditions related to the origin of the request, such as client IP, TLS fingerprint, etc. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher))
- `request_matcher` (Attributes) request_matcher Conditions related to the request, such as query parameters, headers, etc. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--request_matcher))

Optional:

- `api_endpoint` (Attributes) api endpoint path The endpoint (path) of the request. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--destination_type_choice--api_endpoint))

- `base_path` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(128)`

Optional:

- `methods` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["COPY", "POST", "DELETE", "CONNECT", "TRACE", "PATCH", "ANY", "GET", "HEAD", "PUT", "OPTIONS"]` (Multiple selection allowed)

- `specific_domain` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(128)`


<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.client_matcher`

Required:

- `client_choice` (Attributes) client choice Select clients for which the rule will be applied. The actual names of the client are extracted from its TLS certificate. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--client_choice))
- `ip_asn_choice` (Attributes) ip asn choice Match source IP address/ASN  of the request (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice))

Optional:

- `tls_fingerprint_matcher` (Attributes) TLS JA3 fingerprint matcher TLS JA3 fingerprints to be matched. The predicate evaluates to true if the TLS fingerprint matches any of the exact values or classes of known TLS fingerprints. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--tls_fingerprint_matcher))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--client_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.client_matcher.client_choice`

Optional:

- `any_client` (Boolean) any ip Any Client
- `client_selector` (Attributes) client selector A label selector that describes the expected set of clients. The labels associated with the client making the API request are used to evaluate the label expressions in the selector. These labels can be derived from the client TLS certificate or from the volterra internal control plane. This is a more flexible and powerful version of the client name matcher predicate that allows a given rule to be applicable to a set of clients based on the client labels rather than being limited to relying on patterns in the client name. The predicate evaluates to true if the expressions in the label selector are true for the client labels. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--client_selector))
- `ip_threat_category_list` (Attributes) IP Threat Category List IP threat categories to choose from (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--ip_threat_category_list))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--client_selector"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.client_matcher.client_choice.client_selector`

Required:

- `expressions` (List of String) expressions expressions contains the kubernetes style label expression for selections.


<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--ip_threat_category_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.client_matcher.client_choice.ip_threat_category_list`

Required:

- `ip_threat_categories` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["DENIAL_OF_SERVICE", "NETWORK", "SPAM_SOURCES", "WEB_ATTACKS", "BOTNETS", "PROXY", "TOR_PROXY", "WINDOWS_EXPLOITS", "SCANNERS", "REPUTATION", "PHISHING", "MOBILE_THREATS"]` (Multiple selection allowed)



<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.client_matcher.ip_asn_choice`

Optional:

- `any_ip` (Boolean) any ip Any Source IP
- `asn_list` (Attributes) asn list List of 4-byte ASN values. The predicate evaluates to true if the origin ASN is present in the ASN list. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_list))
- `asn_matcher` (Attributes) asn matcher List of references to BGP ASN Set objects. The predicate evaluates to true if the origin ASN is present in one of the BGP ASN Set objects. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_matcher))
- `ip_matcher` (Attributes) ip matcher List of references to IP Prefix Set objects. The predicate evaluates to true if the client IP Address is covered by one or more of the IP Prefixes in the IP Prefix Sets. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--ip_matcher))
- `ip_prefix_list` (Attributes) ip prefix list List of IP Prefixes values. The predicate evaluates to true if the client IP Address is covered by one or more of the IP Prefixes from the list. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--ip_prefix_list))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.client_matcher.ip_asn_choice.asn_list`

Required:

- `as_numbers` (List of Number) as numbers An unordered set of RFC 6793 defined 4-byte AS numbers that can be used to create allow or deny lists for use in network policy or service policy. It can be used to create the allow list only for DNS Load Balancer.


<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.ip_allowed_list_choice.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.bypass_rate_limiting_rules.client_matcher.ip_asn_choice.asn_matcher`

Required:

Optional:

- `cookie_matchers` (Attributes List) cookie matchers A list of predicates for all cookies that need to be matched. The criteria for matching each cookie is described in individual instances of CookieMatcherType. The actual cookie values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each cookie name. Note that all specified cookie matcher predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--request_matcher--cookie_matchers))
- `headers` (Attributes List) headers A list of predicates for various HTTP headers that need to match. The criteria for matching each HTTP header are described in individual HeaderMatcherType instances. The actual HTTP header values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each HTTP header type. Note that all specified header predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--request_matcher--headers))
- `jwt_claims` (Attributes List) JWT claims A list of predicates for various JWT claims that need to match. The criteria for matching each JWT claim are described in individual JWTClaimMatcherType instances. The actual JWT claims values are extracted from the JWT payload as a list of strings. Note that all specified JWT claim predicates must evaluate to true. Note that this feature only works on LBs with JWT Validation feature enabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--request_matcher--jwt_claims))
- `query_params` (Attributes List) query params A list of predicates for all query parameters that need to be matched. The criteria for matching each query parameter are described in individual instances of QueryParameterMatcherType. The actual query parameter values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each query parameter name. Note that all specified query parameter predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--bypass_rate_limiting_rules--request_matcher--query_params))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.client_matcher`

Required:

- `client_choice` (Attributes) client choice Select clients for which the rule will be applied. The actual names of the client are extracted from its TLS certificate. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--client_choice))
- `ip_asn_choice` (Attributes) ip asn choice Match source IP address/ASN  of the request (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice))

Optional:

- `tls_fingerprint_matcher` (Attributes) TLS JA3 fingerprint matcher TLS JA3 fingerprints to be matched. The predicate evaluates to true if the TLS fingerprint matches any of the exact values or classes of known TLS fingerprints. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--tls_fingerprint_matcher))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--client_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.client_matcher.client_choice`

Optional:

- `any_client` (Boolean) any ip Any Client
- `client_selector` (Attributes) client selector A label selector that describes the expected set of clients. The labels associated with the client making the API request are used to evaluate the label expressions in the selector. These labels can be derived from the client TLS certificate or from the volterra internal control plane. This is a more flexible and powerful version of the client name matcher predicate that allows a given rule to be applicable to a set of clients based on the client labels rather than being limited to relying on patterns in the client name. The predicate evaluates to true if the expressions in the label selector are true for the client labels. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--client_selector))
- `ip_threat_category_list` (Attributes) IP Threat Category List IP threat categories to choose from (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--ip_threat_category_list))

Required:

- `expressions` (List of String) expressions expressions contains the kubernetes style label expression for selections.


<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--ip_threat_category_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.client_matcher.client_choice.ip_threat_category_list`

Required:

- `ip_threat_categories` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["REPUTATION", "PHISHING", "MOBILE_THREATS", "DENIAL_OF_SERVICE", "NETWORK", "SPAM_SOURCES", "WEB_ATTACKS", "BOTNETS", "PROXY", "TOR_PROXY", "WINDOWS_EXPLOITS", "SCANNERS"]` (Multiple selection allowed)



<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.client_matcher.ip_asn_choice`

Optional:

- `any_ip` (Boolean) any ip Any Source IP
- `asn_list` (Attributes) asn list List of 4-byte ASN values. The predicate evaluates to true if the origin ASN is present in the ASN list. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_list))
- `asn_matcher` (Attributes) asn matcher List of references to BGP ASN Set objects. The predicate evaluates to true if the origin ASN is present in one of the BGP ASN Set objects. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_matcher))
- `ip_matcher` (Attributes) ip matcher List of references to IP Prefix Set objects. The predicate evaluates to true if the client IP Address is covered by one or more of the IP Prefixes in the IP Prefix Sets. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--ip_matcher))
- `ip_prefix_list` (Attributes) ip prefix list List of IP Prefixes values. The predicate evaluates to true if the client IP Address is covered by one or more of the IP Prefixes from the list. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--ip_prefix_list))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.client_matcher.ip_asn_choice.asn_list`

Required:

- `as_numbers` (List of Number) as numbers An unordered set of RFC 6793 defined 4-byte AS numbers that can be used to create allow or deny lists for use in network policy or service policy. It can be used to create the allow list only for DNS Load Balancer.


<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.client_matcher.ip_asn_choice.asn_matcher`

Required:

### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.client_matcher.ip_asn_choice.ip_prefix_list`

Optional:

- `invert_match` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `ip_prefixes` (List of String) ip prefixes List of IPv4 prefix strings.
- `ipv6_prefixes` (List of String) ipv6 prefixes List of IPv6 prefix strings.



<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--client_matcher--tls_fingerprint_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.client_matcher.tls_fingerprint_matcher`

Optional:

- `classes` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["TOFSEE", "TRICKBOT", "ADWARE", "ADWIND", "GOOTKIT", "GOZI", "JBIFROST", "QUAKBOT", "TROLDESH", "TORRENTLOCKER", "TLS_FINGERPRINT_NONE", "ANY_MALICIOUS_FINGERPRINT", "DRIDEX", "RANSOMWARE"]` (Multiple selection allowed)
- `exact_values` (List of String) exact values A list of exact TLS JA3 fingerprints to match the input TLS JA3 fingerprint against.
- `excluded_values` (List of String) excluded values A list of TLS JA3 fingerprints to be excluded when matching the input TLS JA3 fingerprint. This can be used to skip known false positives when using one or more known TLS fingerprint classes in the enclosing matcher.



<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.request_matcher`

Optional:

- `cookie_matchers` (Attributes List) cookie matchers A list of predicates for all cookies that need to be matched. The criteria for matching each cookie is described in individual instances of CookieMatcherType. The actual cookie values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each cookie name. Note that all specified cookie matcher predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher--cookie_matchers))
- `headers` (Attributes List) headers A list of predicates for various HTTP headers that need to match. The criteria for matching each HTTP header are described in individual HeaderMatcherType instances. The actual HTTP header values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each HTTP header type. Note that all specified header predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher--headers))
- `jwt_claims` (Attributes List) JWT claims A list of predicates for various JWT claims that need to match. The criteria for matching each JWT claim are described in individual JWTClaimMatcherType instances. The actual JWT claims values are extracted from the JWT payload as a list of strings. Note that all specified JWT claim predicates must evaluate to true. Note that this feature only works on LBs with JWT Validation feature enabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher--jwt_claims))
- `query_params` (Attributes List) query params A list of predicates for all query parameters that need to be matched. The criteria for matching each query parameter are described in individual instances of QueryParameterMatcherType. The actual query parameter values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each query parameter name. Note that all specified query parameter predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher--query_params))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher--cookie_matchers"></a>

Required:

- `match_choice` (Attributes) match Select Match Options. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher--cookie_matchers--match_choice))
- `name` (String) name A case-sensitive cookie name.

Optional:

- `invert_matcher` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher--cookie_matchers--match_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.api_endpoint_rules.request_matcher.cookie_matchers.match_choice`

Optional:

- `check_not_present` (Boolean) check_not_present Check that the cookie is not present.
- `check_present` (Boolean) check_present Check that the cookie is present.
- `item` (Attributes) item Criteria for matching the values for the cookie. The match is successful if any of the values in the input satisfies the criteria in the matcher. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--api_endpoint_rules--request_matcher--cookie_matchers--match_choice--item))

- `regex_values` (List of String) regex values A list of regular expressions to match the input against.
- `transformers` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["URL_DECODE", "TRIM_LEFT", "LOWER_CASE", "NORMALIZE_PATH", "REMOVE_WHITESPACE", "TRIM_RIGHT", "TRIM", "TRANSFORMER_NONE", "UPPER_CASE", "BASE64_DECODE"]` (Multiple selection allowed)

- `rate_limiter_choice` (Attributes) Rate limit values This defines the values of the rate limiter. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--rate_limiter_choice))

- `request_matcher` (Attributes) request_matcher Conditions related to the request, such as query parameters, headers, etc. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--request_matcher))

- `ref_rate_limiter` (Attributes) External Rate Limiter Use external rate limiter. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--rate_limiter_choice--ref_rate_limiter))

- `unit` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["HOUR", "DAY", "SECOND", "MINUTE"]`

Optional:

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.server_url_rules.client_matcher`

Required:

- `client_choice` (Attributes) client choice Select clients for which the rule will be applied. The actual names of the client are extracted from its TLS certificate. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--client_choice))
- `ip_asn_choice` (Attributes) ip asn choice Match source IP address/ASN  of the request (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice))

Optional:

- `tls_fingerprint_matcher` (Attributes) TLS JA3 fingerprint matcher TLS JA3 fingerprints to be matched. The predicate evaluates to true if the TLS fingerprint matches any of the exact values or classes of known TLS fingerprints. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--tls_fingerprint_matcher))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--client_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.server_url_rules.client_matcher.client_choice`

Optional:

- `any_client` (Boolean) any ip Any Client
- `client_selector` (Attributes) client selector A label selector that describes the expected set of clients. The labels associated with the client making the API request are used to evaluate the label expressions in the selector. These labels can be derived from the client TLS certificate or from the volterra internal control plane. This is a more flexible and powerful version of the client name matcher predicate that allows a given rule to be applicable to a set of clients based on the client labels rather than being limited to relying on patterns in the client name. The predicate evaluates to true if the expressions in the label selector are true for the client labels. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--client_selector))
- `ip_threat_category_list` (Attributes) IP Threat Category List IP threat categories to choose from (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--ip_threat_category_list))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--client_selector"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.server_url_rules.client_matcher.client_choice.client_selector`

Required:

- `expressions` (List of String) expressions expressions contains the kubernetes style label expression for selections.


<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--client_choice--ip_threat_category_list"></a>

- `asn_matcher` (Attributes) asn matcher List of references to BGP ASN Set objects. The predicate evaluates to true if the origin ASN is present in one of the BGP ASN Set objects. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--asn_matcher))

- `ip_prefix_list` (Attributes) ip prefix list List of IP Prefixes values. The predicate evaluates to true if the client IP Address is covered by one or more of the IP Prefixes from the list. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--ip_asn_choice--ip_prefix_list))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--client_matcher--tls_fingerprint_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.server_url_rules.client_matcher.tls_fingerprint_matcher`

Optional:

- `classes` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["ADWARE", "ADWIND", "GOOTKIT", "GOZI", "JBIFROST", "QUAKBOT", "TROLDESH", "TORRENTLOCKER", "TLS_FINGERPRINT_NONE", "ANY_MALICIOUS_FINGERPRINT", "DRIDEX", "RANSOMWARE", "TOFSEE", "TRICKBOT"]` (Multiple selection allowed)
- `exact_values` (List of String) exact values A list of exact TLS JA3 fingerprints to match the input TLS JA3 fingerprint against.
- `excluded_values` (List of String) excluded values A list of TLS JA3 fingerprints to be excluded when matching the input TLS JA3 fingerprint. This can be used to skip known false positives when using one or more known TLS fingerprint classes in the enclosing matcher.



<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--request_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.api_rate_limit.server_url_rules.request_matcher`

Optional:

- `cookie_matchers` (Attributes List) cookie matchers A list of predicates for all cookies that need to be matched. The criteria for matching each cookie is described in individual instances of CookieMatcherType. The actual cookie values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each cookie name. Note that all specified cookie matcher predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--request_matcher--cookie_matchers))
- `headers` (Attributes List) headers A list of predicates for various HTTP headers that need to match. The criteria for matching each HTTP header are described in individual HeaderMatcherType instances. The actual HTTP header values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each HTTP header type. Note that all specified header predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--request_matcher--headers))
- `jwt_claims` (Attributes List) JWT claims A list of predicates for various JWT claims that need to match. The criteria for matching each JWT claim are described in individual JWTClaimMatcherType instances. The actual JWT claims values are extracted from the JWT payload as a list of strings. Note that all specified JWT claim predicates must evaluate to true. Note that this feature only works on LBs with JWT Validation feature enabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--request_matcher--jwt_claims))
- `query_params` (Attributes List) query params A list of predicates for all query parameters that need to be matched. The criteria for matching each query parameter are described in individual instances of QueryParameterMatcherType. The actual query parameter values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each query parameter name. Note that all specified query parameter predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--api_rate_limit--server_url_rules--request_matcher--query_params))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.rate_limit`

Required:

- `ip_allowed_list_choice` (Attributes) IP Allowed List Allowed List of IPs for which rate limiting will be disabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice))
- `policy_choice` (Attributes) policy choice Manage rate limiter policies. Note that an implicit policy based on the IP Allowed List is always applied as the first policy. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit--policy_choice))

Optional:

- `rate_limiter` (Attributes) rate_limiter Requests to the virtual_host are rate limited based on the parameters specified in the rate_limiter. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit--rate_limiter))

<a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice"></a>

Optional:

- `custom_ip_allowed_list` (Attributes) Custom IP list IP Allowed list using existing ip_prefix_set objects. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--custom_ip_allowed_list))
- `ip_allowed_list` (Attributes) IP Allowed List List of IP(s) for which rate limiting will be disabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit--ip_allowed_list_choice--ip_allowed_list))
- `no_ip_allowed_list` (Boolean) No IP Allowed List There is no ip allowed list for rate limiting, all clients go through rate limiting.

Requests from source IP addresses that are covered by one of the allowed IP Prefixes are not subjected to rate limiting. <a id="nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit--policy_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `rate_limit_choice.rate_limit.policy_choice`

Optional:

- `no_policies` (Boolean) no_policies Do not apply additional rate limiter policies.
- `policies` (Attributes) policies Apply rate limiter policies to requests that are not from the IP Allowed List. The policies are evaluated sequentially until a matching rule is identified. If there's a match, the rate limiting behavior is modified according to the action configured in the rule. If there's no match, the rate limiting configuration for the HTTP load balancer is honored. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--rate_limit_choice--rate_limit--policy_choice--policies))

<a id="nestedatt--service_policy_choice"></a>

- `no_service_policies` (Boolean) Do Not Apply Service Policies Do not apply any service policies i.e. bypass the namespace service policy set
- `service_policies_from_namespace` (Boolean) Apply Namespace Service Policies Apply the active service policies configured as part of the namespace service policy set

<a id="nestedatt--service_policy_choice--active_service_policies"></a>
### Nested Schema for `service_policy_choice.active_service_policies`

Required:

- `policies` (Attributes List) policies Service Policies is a sequential engine where policies (and rules within the policy) are evaluated one after the other. It's important to define the correct order (policies evaluated from top to bottom in the list) for service policies, to get the intended result. For each request, its characteristics are evaluated based on the match criteria in each service policy starting at the top. If there is a match in the current policy, then the policy takes effect, and no more policies are evaluated. Otherwise, the next policy is evaluated. If all policies are evaluated and none match, then the request will be denied by default. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--service_policy_choice--active_service_policies--policies))

<a id="nestedatt--service_policy_choice--active_service_policies--policies"></a>
### Nested Schema for `service_policy_choice.active_service_policies.policies`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)

### Nested Schema for `threat_mesh_choice`

<a id="nestedatt--trust_client_ip_headers_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `trust_client_ip_headers_choice`

Optional:

- `disable_trust_client_ip_headers` (Boolean) Disable
- `enable_trust_client_ip_headers` (Attributes) Enable (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--trust_client_ip_headers_choice--enable_trust_client_ip_headers))

<a id="nestedatt--trust_client_ip_headers_choice--enable_trust_client_ip_headers"></a>
### Nested Schema for `trust_client_ip_headers_choice.enable_trust_client_ip_headers`

Required:

- `client_ip_headers` (List of String) Client IP Headers Define the list of one or more Client IP Headers.  Headers will be used in order from top to bottom, meaning if the first header is not present in the request, the system will proceed to check for the second header, and so on, until one of the listed headers is found. If none of the defined headers exist, or the value is not an IP address, then the system will use the source IP of the packet. If multiple defined headers with different names are present in the request, the value of the first header name in the configuration will be used. If multiple defined headers with the same name are present in the request, values of all those headers will be combined. The system will read the right-most IP address from header, if there are multiple ip addresses in the header value. For X-Forwarded-For header, the system will read the IP address(rightmost - 1), as the client ip



<a id="nestedatt--user_id_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `user_id_choice`

Optional:

- `user_id_client_ip` (Boolean) user_id_client_ip Use the Client IP address as the user identifier.
- `user_identification` (Attributes) user_identification A reference to user_identification object. The rules in the user_identification object are evaluated to determine the user identifier. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--user_id_choice--user_identification))

- `namespace` (String)

<a id="nestedatt--blocked_clients"></a>
### Nested Schema for `blocked_clients`

Required:

- `client_source_choice` (Attributes) Client Identifier Client identifier that should be used for the client rule (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--blocked_clients--client_source_choice))
- `metadata` (Attributes) metadata Common attributes for the rule including name and description. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--blocked_clients--metadata))

Optional:

- `actions` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["SKIP_PROCESSING_WAF", "SKIP_PROCESSING_MUM", "SKIP_PROCESSING_API_PROTECTION", "SKIP_PROCESSING_OAS_VALIDATION", "SKIP_PROCESSING_DDOS_PROTECTION", "SKIP_PROCESSING_MALWARE_PROTECTION", "SKIP_PROCESSING_BOT", "SKIP_PROCESSING_IP_REPUTATION", "SKIP_PROCESSING_THREAT_MESH"]` (Multiple selection allowed)
- `expiration_timestamp` (String) expiration timestamp The expiration_timestamp is the RFC 3339 format timestamp at which the containing rule is considered to be logically expired. The rule continues to exist in the configuration but is not applied anymore.

<a id="nestedatt--blocked_clients--client_source_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `blocked_clients.client_source_choice`

Optional:

- `as_number` (Number) as number RFC 6793 defined 4-byte AS number
- `http_header` (Attributes) HTTP Header Request header name and value pairs (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--blocked_clients--client_source_choice--http_header))
- `ip_prefix` (String) ip prefix IPv4 prefix string.
- `ipv6_prefix` (String) ipv6 prefix IPv6 prefix string.
- `user_identifier` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`

<a id="nestedatt--blocked_clients--client_source_choice--http_header"></a>
### Nested Schema for `blocked_clients.client_source_choice.http_header`

Required:

- `headers` (Attributes List) headers List of HTTP header name and value pairs (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--blocked_clients--client_source_choice--http_header--headers))

<a id="nestedatt--blocked_clients--client_source_choice--http_header--headers"></a>
### Nested Schema for `blocked_clients.client_source_choice.http_header.headers`

Required:

- `name` (String) name Name of the header

Optional:

- `invert_match` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `value_match_choice` (Attributes) value_match The variants of matching a header value (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--blocked_clients--client_source_choice--http_header--headers--value_match_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--blocked_clients--client_source_choice--http_header--headers--value_match_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `blocked_clients.client_source_choice.http_header.headers.value_match_choice`

Optional:

- `exact` (String) exact Header value to match exactly
- `presence` (Boolean) presence If true, check for presence of header
- `regex` (String) regex Regex match of the header value in re2 format

- `check_present` (Boolean) check_present Check that the header is present.
- `item` (Attributes) item Criteria for matching the values for the header. The match is successful if any of the values in the input satisfies the criteria in the matcher. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--mobile_sdk_choice--mobile_sdk_config--mobile_identifier--headers--match_choice--item))

### Nested Schema for `bot_defense_choice.bot_defense.policy.mobile_sdk_choice.mobile_sdk_config.mobile_identifier.headers.match_choice.item`

Optional:

- `exact_values` (List of String) exact values A list of exact values to match the input against.
- `regex_values` (List of String) regex values A list of regular expressions to match the input against.
- `transformers` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["LOWER_CASE", "NORMALIZE_PATH", "REMOVE_WHITESPACE", "TRIM_RIGHT", "TRIM", "TRANSFORMER_NONE", "UPPER_CASE", "BASE64_DECODE", "URL_DECODE", "TRIM_LEFT"]` (Multiple selection allowed)







<a id="nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints"></a>
### Nested Schema for `bot_defense_choice.bot_defense.policy.protected_app_endpoints`

Required:

- `app_traffic_type_choice` (Attributes) Traffic channel Select traffic channel. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--app_traffic_type_choice))
- `flow_label_choice` (Attributes) Flow label Endpoint label category (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--flow_label_choice))
- `goodbot_choice` (Attributes) Good Bot The system will distinguish between good bot traffic and malicious automation. Select if you want to allow good bots to continue to the origin or receive the same mitigation handling as defined for all automated traffic. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--goodbot_choice))
- `http_methods` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["METHOD_ANY", "METHOD_GET", "METHOD_POST", "METHOD_PUT", "METHOD_PATCH", "METHOD_DELETE", "METHOD_GET_DOCUMENT"]` (Multiple selection allowed)
- `metadata` (Attributes) metadata Common attributes for the rule including name and description. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--metadata))
- `mitigation` (Attributes) Mitigation Mitigation action. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--mitigation))
- `path` (Attributes) Path Matching URI path of the route. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--path))

Optional:

- `domain_matcher_choice` (Attributes) Domain Matcher This defines domain matcher. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--domain_matcher_choice))
- `headers` (Attributes List) headers A list of predicates for various HTTP headers that need to match. The criteria for matching each HTTP header are described in individual HeaderMatcherType instances. The actual HTTP header values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each HTTP header type. Note that all specified header predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--headers))
- `protocol` (List of String) <br>**Allowed Values (choose one):** `["BOTH", "HTTP", "HTTPS"]`
- `query_params` (Attributes List) query params A list of predicates for all query parameters that need to be matched. The criteria for matching each query parameter are described in individual instances of QueryParameterMatcherType. The actual query parameter values are extracted from the request API as a list of strings for each query parameter name. Note that all specified query parameter predicates must evaluate to true. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--bot_defense_choice--bot_defense--policy--protected_app_endpoints--query_params))

Required:

- `metadata` (Attributes) metadata Common attributes for the rule including name and description. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--ddos_mitigation_rules--metadata))

Optional:

- `expiration_timestamp` (String) expiration timestamp The expiration_timestamp is the RFC 3339 format timestamp at which the containing rule is considered to be logically expired. The rule continues to exist in the configuration but is not applied anymore.

"COUNTRY_FM", "COUNTRY_PE", "COUNTRY_TK", "COUNTRY_CS", "COUNTRY_JP", "COUNTRY_KM", "COUNTRY_SB", "COUNTRY_VA", "COUNTRY_AD", "COUNTRY_HR", "COUNTRY_KN", "COUNTRY_ZW", "COUNTRY_AT", "COUNTRY_ME", "COUNTRY_MX", "COUNTRY_NE", "COUNTRY_NO", "COUNTRY_VG", "COUNTRY_GH", "COUNTRY_LT"]` (Multiple selection allowed)
- `ja4_tls_fingerprint_matcher` (Attributes) TLS JA4 fingerprint matcher TLS JA4 fingerprints to be matched. The predicate evaluates to true if source JA4 TLS fingerprint matches any of the exact values of JA4 TLS fingerprints. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--ddos_mitigation_rules--mitigation_choice--ddos_client_source--ja4_tls_fingerprint_matcher))
- `tls_fingerprint_matcher` (Attributes) TLS JA3 fingerprint matcher TLS JA3 fingerprints to be matched. The predicate evaluates to true if the TLS fingerprint matches any of the exact values or classes of known TLS fingerprints. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--ddos_mitigation_rules--mitigation_choice--ddos_client_source--tls_fingerprint_matcher))

<a id="nestedatt--ddos_mitigation_rules--mitigation_choice--ddos_client_source--asn_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `ddos_mitigation_rules.mitigation_choice.ddos_client_source.asn_list`

Required:

- `as_numbers` (List of Number) as numbers An unordered set of RFC 6793 defined 4-byte AS numbers that can be used to create allow or deny lists for use in network policy or service policy. It can be used to create the allow list only for DNS Load Balancer.


<a id="nestedatt--ddos_mitigation_rules--mitigation_choice--ddos_client_source--ja4_tls_fingerprint_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `ddos_mitigation_rules.mitigation_choice.ddos_client_source.ja4_tls_fingerprint_matcher`

Optional:

- `exact_values` (List of String) exact values A list of exact JA4 TLS fingerprint to match the input JA4 TLS fingerprint against


<a id="nestedatt--ddos_mitigation_rules--mitigation_choice--ddos_client_source--tls_fingerprint_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `ddos_mitigation_rules.mitigation_choice.ddos_client_source.tls_fingerprint_matcher`

Optional:

- `classes` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["ADWARE", "ADWIND", "GOOTKIT", "GOZI", "JBIFROST", "QUAKBOT", "TROLDESH", "TORRENTLOCKER", "TLS_FINGERPRINT_NONE", "ANY_MALICIOUS_FINGERPRINT", "DRIDEX", "RANSOMWARE", "TOFSEE", "TRICKBOT"]` (Multiple selection allowed)
- `exact_values` (List of String) exact values A list of exact TLS JA3 fingerprints to match the input TLS JA3 fingerprint against.
- `excluded_values` (List of String) excluded values A list of TLS JA3 fingerprints to be excluded when matching the input TLS JA3 fingerprint. This can be used to skip known false positives when using one or more known TLS fingerprint classes in the enclosing matcher.



<a id="nestedatt--ddos_mitigation_rules--mitigation_choice--ip_prefix_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `ddos_mitigation_rules.mitigation_choice.ip_prefix_list`

Optional:

- `invert_match` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `ip_prefixes` (List of String) ip prefixes List of IPv4 prefix strings.
- `ipv6_prefixes` (List of String) ipv6 prefixes List of IPv6 prefix strings.




<a id="nestedatt--default_route_pools"></a>
### Nested Schema for `default_route_pools`

Required:

- `pool_choice` (Attributes) Pool Choice Select origin pool method, simple origin pool method or more flexible cluster object. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--default_route_pools--pool_choice))

Optional:

- `endpoint_subsets` (Map of String) Origin Servers Subset Upstream origin pool may be configured to divide its origin servers into subsets based on metadata attached to the origin servers. Routes may then specify the metadata that a endpoint must match in order to be selected by the load balancer For origin servers which are discovered in K8S or Consul cluster, the label of the service is merged with endpoint's labels. In case of Consul, the label is derived from the 'Tag' field. For labels that are common between configured endpoint and discovered service, labels from discovered service takes precedence. List of key-value pairs that will be used as matching metadata. Only those origin servers of upstream origin pool which match this metadata will be selected for load balancing
- `priority` (Number) <br>**Default:** `1`
- `weight` (Number) <br>**Default:** `1`

<a id="nestedatt--default_route_pools--pool_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `default_route_pools.pool_choice`

Optional:

- `cluster` (Attributes) Cluster More flexible, advanced feature control with cluster (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--default_route_pools--pool_choice--cluster))
- `pool` (Attributes) Pool Simple, commonly used pool parameters with origin pool (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--default_route_pools--pool_choice--pool))

<a id="nestedatt--default_route_pools--pool_choice--cluster"></a>
### Nested Schema for `default_route_pools.pool_choice.cluster`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)


<a id="nestedatt--default_route_pools--pool_choice--pool"></a>
### Nested Schema for `default_route_pools.pool_choice.pool`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)

<a id="nestedatt--protected_cookies"></a>
### Nested Schema for `protected_cookies`

Required:

- `cookie_tampering_choice` (Attributes) cookie_tampering Cookie Tampering Protection prevents attackers from modifying the value of session cookies. To use this feature, Web Application Firewall must be enabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--protected_cookies--cookie_tampering_choice))
- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`

Optional:

- `httponly_choice` (Attributes) httponly A cookie with the HttpOnly attribute is inaccessible to the JavaScript. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--protected_cookies--httponly_choice))
- `samesite_choice` (Attributes) samesite Controls whether or not a cookie is sent with cross-site requests (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--protected_cookies--samesite_choice))
- `secure_choice` (Attributes) secure A cookie with the Secure attribute is only sent to the server with an encrypted request over the HTTPS protocol and therefore, is more resistant to man-in-the-middle attacks. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--protected_cookies--secure_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--protected_cookies--cookie_tampering_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `protected_cookies.cookie_tampering_choice`

- `enable_tampering_protection` (Boolean) enable

### Nested Schema for `protected_cookies.httponly_choice`

Optional:

- `add_httponly` (Boolean) add_httponly
- `ignore_httponly` (Boolean) ignore_httponly


<a id="nestedatt--protected_cookies--samesite_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `protected_cookies.samesite_choice`

Optional:

- `ignore_samesite` (Boolean) ignore_samesite Ignore Samesite attribute
- `samesite_lax` (Boolean) lax Add Samesite attribute with Lax. Means that the cookie is not sent on cross-site requests
- `samesite_none` (Boolean) none Add Samesite attribute with None. Means that the browser sends the cookie with both cross-site and same-site requests
- `samesite_strict` (Boolean) strict Add Samesite attribute with Strict. Means that the browser sends the cookie only for same-site requests


<a id="nestedatt--protected_cookies--secure_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `protected_cookies.secure_choice`

Optional:

- `add_secure` (Boolean) add_secure
- `ignore_secure` (Boolean) ignore_secure



<a id="nestedatt--routes"></a>
### Nested Schema for `routes`

Required:

- `choice` (Attributes) choice Define a simple, redirect, direct response or custom route (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--routes--choice))
- `route_state_choice` (Attributes) Route Activation By default routes are enabled after saving. Use disabled state for work in progress routes, or temporary turn off specific routes. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--routes--route_state_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--routes--choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `routes.choice`

Optional:

- `custom_route_object` (Attributes) RouteTypeCustomRoute A custom route uses a route object created outside of this view. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--routes--choice--custom_route_object))
- `direct_response_route` (Attributes) RouteTypeDirectResponse A direct response route matches on path and/or HTTP method and responds directly to the matching traffic (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--routes--choice--direct_response_route))
- `redirect_route` (Attributes) RouteTypeRedirect A redirect route matches on path and/or HTTP method and redirects the matching traffic to a different URL (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--routes--choice--redirect_route))
- `simple_route` (Attributes) RouteTypeSimple A simple route matches on path and/or HTTP method and forwards the matching traffic to the associated pools (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--routes--choice--simple_route))

- `route_ref` (Attributes) route_refs Reference to a custom route object (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--routes--choice--custom_route_object--route_ref))
