- `domains` (List of String) Domains A list of domain names that will be matched to loadbalancer. These domains are not used for SNI match. Wildcard names are supported in the suffix or prefix form.




<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation`

Required:

- `action` (Attributes) action (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--action))
- `jwks_configuration_choice` (Attributes) jwks_configuration Configuration of the encryption keys that are used for JWT signature validation. You can enter the JWKS locally or use a defined Authorization Server to remotely fetch JWKS automatically. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--jwks_configuration_choice))
- `target` (Attributes) target Define endpoints for which JWT token validation will be performed (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--target))
- `token_location` (Attributes) token location Define where in the HTTP request the JWT token will be extracted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--token_location))

Optional:

- `mandatory_claims` (Attributes) Mandatory Claims Configuration for validation of mandatory claims. The system will verify that the claim exists in JWT. If the claim does not exist JWT token validation will fail. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--mandatory_claims))
- `reserved_claims` (Attributes) reserved_claims Configuration required for validation of reserved claims. If some claims are absent in the token validation of these claims should be disabled. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims))

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--action"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.action`

Required:

- `action_choice` (Attributes) action Action to be taken (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--action--action_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--action--action_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.action.action_choice`

Optional:

- `block` (Boolean) block Block the request and report the issue
- `report` (Boolean) report Allow the request and report the issue



<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--jwks_configuration_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.jwks_configuration_choice`

Optional:

- `authorization_server` (Attributes) authorization_server Automatically fetch JWKS from URI endpoint (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--jwks_configuration_choice--authorization_server))
- `jwks_config` (Attributes) jwks_config Manually provide JWKS (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--jwks_configuration_choice--jwks_config))

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--jwks_configuration_choice--authorization_server"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.jwks_configuration_choice.authorization_server`

Required:

- `authorization_servers` (Attributes List) authorization_server_name Authorization Servers are configured separately in the 'Shared Objects' section of the Web App & API Protection workspace and used to fetch JWKS for JWT validation. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--jwks_configuration_choice--authorization_server--authorization_servers))

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--jwks_configuration_choice--authorization_server--authorization_servers"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.jwks_configuration_choice.authorization_server.authorization_servers`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)



<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--jwks_configuration_choice--jwks_config"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.jwks_configuration_choice.jwks_config`

Optional:

- `cleartext` (String) cleartext The JSON Web Key Set (JWKS) is a set of keys used to verify JSON Web Token (JWT) issued by the Authorization Server. See RFC 7517 for more details.



<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--target"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.target`

Required:

- `target_choice` (Attributes) target Define endpoints for which JWT token validation will be performed (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--target--target_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--target--target_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.target.target_choice`

Optional:

- `all_endpoint` (Boolean) all_endpoint Validation will be performed for all requests on this LB
- `api_groups` (Attributes) api group Validation will be performed for the endpoints mentioned in the API Groups (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--target--target_choice--api_groups))
- `base_paths` (Attributes) base paths Validation will be performed for selected path prefixes (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--target--target_choice--base_paths))

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--target--target_choice--api_groups"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.target.target_choice.api_groups`

Required:

- `api_groups` (List of String) api group


<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--target--target_choice--base_paths"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.target.target_choice.base_paths`

Required:

- `base_paths` (List of String) base_paths




<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--token_location"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.token_location`

Required:

- `token_location_choice` (Attributes) token location Define where in the HTTP request the JWT token will be extracted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--token_location--token_location_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--token_location--token_location_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.token_location.token_location_choice`

Optional:

- `bearer_token` (Boolean) bearer token Token is found in Authorization HTTP header with Bearer authentication scheme



<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--mandatory_claims"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.mandatory_claims`

Optional:

- `claim_names` (List of String) Claim names


<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.reserved_claims`

Required:

- `audience_validation_choice` (Attributes) audience_validation The value of the aud claim in the JWT token should match one of the configured values (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims--audience_validation_choice))
- `issuer_validation_choice` (Attributes) issuer_validation The value of the iss claim in the JWT token should exactly match the configured value (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims--issuer_validation_choice))
- `validate_period_choice` (Attributes) validate_period Validate that current time is before the Expiration Time (as specified in the exp claim). If exp claim is not present JWT validation fails. If Not Before (nbf) claim is present in the JWT token validate that current time is after the nbf value. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims--validate_period_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims--audience_validation_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.reserved_claims.audience_validation_choice`

Optional:

- `audience` (Attributes) audience (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims--audience_validation_choice--audience))
- `audience_disable` (Boolean) audience_disable

<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims--audience_validation_choice--audience"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.reserved_claims.audience_validation_choice.audience`

Required:

- `audiences` (List of String) audiences



<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims--issuer_validation_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.reserved_claims.issuer_validation_choice`

Optional:

- `issuer` (String) issuer
- `issuer_disable` (Boolean) issuer_disable


<a id="nestedatt--jwt_validation--reserved_claims--validate_period_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `jwt_validation.reserved_claims.validate_period_choice`

Optional:

- `validate_period_disable` (Boolean) validate_period_disable
- `validate_period_enable` (Boolean) validate_period_enable




<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection`

Required:

- `clientside_action_choice` (Attributes) Client-Side Challenge Configure client-side challenge settings for incoming requests (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--clientside_action_choice))
- `ddos_policy_choice` (Attributes) Custom Service Policy Select a service policy that will be activated only during an ongoing DDoS attack. This policy will be executed in addition and after internal L7 DDoS Auto Mitigation Rules, but before all other service policies. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--ddos_policy_choice))
- `mitigation_action_choice` (Attributes) Mitigation Action Select a technique to apply on sources which are detected as suspicious by L7 DDoS detection mechanism (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--mitigation_action_choice))
- `rps_threshold_choice` (Attributes) RPS Threshold Choice An L7 DDoS attack is identified based on service degradation indicators combined with elevated RPS levels. Configure the RPS threshold that is considered high for the server. By default, this is set to 10,000 RPS. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--rps_threshold_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--clientside_action_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.clientside_action_choice`

Optional:

- `clientside_action_captcha_challenge` (Attributes) CAPTCHA Challenge Serve CAPTCHA challenge to all sources (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--clientside_action_choice--clientside_action_captcha_challenge))
- `clientside_action_js_challenge` (Attributes) JavaScript Challenge Serve JavaScript challenge to all sources (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--clientside_action_choice--clientside_action_js_challenge))
- `clientside_action_none` (Boolean) None Don't apply any challenge

<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--clientside_action_choice--clientside_action_captcha_challenge"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.clientside_action_choice.clientside_action_captcha_challenge`

Optional:

- `cookie_expiry` (Number) <br>**Default:** `3600`
- `custom_page` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(65536)`


<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--clientside_action_choice--clientside_action_js_challenge"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.clientside_action_choice.clientside_action_js_challenge`

Optional:

- `cookie_expiry` (Number) <br>**Default:** `3600`
- `custom_page` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(65536)`
- `js_script_delay` (Number) <br>**Default:** `5000`



<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--ddos_policy_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.ddos_policy_choice`

Optional:

- `ddos_policy_custom` (Attributes) Custom Apply a custom service policy during an ongoing DDoS attack (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--ddos_policy_choice--ddos_policy_custom))
- `ddos_policy_none` (Boolean) None Do not apply additional service policy during an ongoing DDoS attack

<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--ddos_policy_choice--ddos_policy_custom"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.ddos_policy_choice.ddos_policy_custom`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)



<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--mitigation_action_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.mitigation_action_choice`

Optional:

- `mitigation_block` (Boolean) Block Block suspicious sources
- `mitigation_captcha_challenge` (Attributes) CAPTCHA Challenge Serve CAPTCHA challenge to suspicious sources (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--mitigation_action_choice--mitigation_captcha_challenge))
- `mitigation_js_challenge` (Attributes) JavaScript Challenge Serve JavaScript challenge to suspicious sources (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--mitigation_action_choice--mitigation_js_challenge))

<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--mitigation_action_choice--mitigation_captcha_challenge"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.mitigation_action_choice.mitigation_captcha_challenge`

Optional:

- `cookie_expiry` (Number) <br>**Default:** `3600`
- `custom_page` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(65536)`


<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--mitigation_action_choice--mitigation_js_challenge"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.mitigation_action_choice.mitigation_js_challenge`

Optional:

- `cookie_expiry` (Number) <br>**Default:** `3600`
- `custom_page` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(65536)`
- `js_script_delay` (Number) <br>**Default:** `5000`



<a id="nestedatt--l7_ddos_protection--rps_threshold_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `l7_ddos_protection.rps_threshold_choice`

Optional:

- `default_rps_threshold` (Boolean) Default RPS Threshold Use the system's default RPS threshold, which is set to 10,000 RPS
- `rps_threshold` (Number) <br>**Default:** `10000`



<a id="nestedatt--more_option"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option`

Required:

- `max_requests_per_connection_choice` (Attributes) max_requests_per_connection Sets the maximum number of requests a downstream client can send over a single connection to Envoy. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--max_requests_per_connection_choice))

Optional:

- `buffer_policy` (Attributes) Buffer configuration for requests Some upstream applications are not capable of handling streamed data and high network latency. This config enables buffering the entire request before sending to upstream application. We can specify the maximum buffer size and buffer interval with this config. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--buffer_policy))
- `compression_params` (Attributes) Compression configuration Enables load balancer to compress dispatched data from an upstream service upon client request. Only GZIP compression is supported (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--compression_params))
- `custom_errors` (Map of String) Custom Errors Map of integer error codes as keys and string values that can be used to provide custom http pages for each error code. Key of the map can be either response code class or HTTP Error code. Response code classes for key is configured as follows 3 -- for 3xx response code class 4 -- for 4xx response code class 5 -- for 5xx response code class Value of the map is string which represents custom HTTP responses. Specific response code takes preference when both response code and response code class matches for a request.
- `disable_default_error_pages` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `idle_timeout` (Number) <br>**Default:** `30000`
- `max_request_header_size` (Number) <br>**Default:** `60`
- `request_cookies_to_add` (Attributes List) Cookies to add in request Cookies are key-value pairs to be added to HTTP request being routed towards upstream. Cookies specified at this level are applied after cookies from matched Route are applied (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add))
- `request_cookies_to_remove` (List of String) Cookies to be removed from request List of keys of Cookies to be removed from the HTTP request being sent towards upstream.
- `request_headers_to_add` (Attributes List) Headers to add in request Headers are key-value pairs to be added to HTTP request being routed towards upstream. Headers specified at this level are applied after headers from matched Route are applied (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add))
- `request_headers_to_remove` (List of String) Header to be removed from request List of keys of Headers to be removed from the HTTP request being sent towards upstream.
- `response_cookies_to_add` (Attributes List) Cookies to add in set-cookie header in response Cookies are name-value pairs along with optional attribute parameters to be added to HTTP response being sent towards downstream. Cookies specified at this level are applied after cookies from matched Route are applied (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add))
- `response_cookies_to_remove` (List of String) Cookies to be removed from response List of name of Cookies to be removed from the HTTP response being sent towards downstream. Entire set-cookie header will be removed
- `response_headers_to_add` (Attributes List) Headers to add in response Headers are key-value pairs to be added to HTTP response being sent towards downstream. Headers specified at this level are applied after headers from matched Route are applied (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_headers_to_add))
- `response_headers_to_remove` (List of String) Header to be removed from response List of keys of Headers to be removed from the HTTP response being sent towards downstream.

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--max_requests_per_connection_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.max_requests_per_connection_choice`

Optional:

- `max_requests_per_connection` (Number) <br>**Default:** `1000`
- `no_request_limit_per_connection` (Boolean) Disable the max requests per connection option This option disables the maximum requests per connection limit. When selected, no limit is enforced, and connections can handle unlimited requests.


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--buffer_policy"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.buffer_policy`

Optional:

- `disabled` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `max_request_bytes` (Number) max_request_bytes The maximum request size that the filter will buffer before the connection manager will stop buffering and return a RequestEntityTooLarge (413) response.


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--compression_params"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.compression_params`

Optional:

- `content_length` (Number) content_length Minimum response length, in bytes, which will trigger compression. The default value is 30.
- `content_type` (List of String) content_type Set of strings that allows specifying which mime-types yield compression When this field is not defined, compression will be applied to the following mime-types: 'application/javascript' 'application/json', 'application/xhtml+xml' 'image/svg+xml' 'text/css' 'text/html' 'text/plain' 'text/xml'
- `disable_on_etag_header` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `remove_accept_encoding_header` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_cookies_to_add`

Required:

- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`
- `value_choice` (Attributes) value Enter Cookie value as string or blindfolded secret. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice))

Optional:

- `overwrite` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_cookies_to_add.value_choice`

Optional:

- `secret_value` (Attributes) Secret Value Secret Value of the Cookie header (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value))
- `value` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(8096)`

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_cookies_to_add.value_choice.secret_value`

Required:

- `secret_info_choice` (Attributes) SecretInfoOneof Secret can be one of the following types - * Blindfold Secret - Secret is managed by F5XC Secret Management System * Clear Secret - Secret is not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_cookies_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice`

Optional:

- `blindfold_secret_info` (Attributes) Blindfold Secret Blindfold Secret is used for the secrets managed by F5XC Secret Management Service (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--blindfold_secret_info))
- `clear_secret_info` (Attributes) Clear Secret Clear Secret is used for the secrets that are not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info))

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--blindfold_secret_info"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_cookies_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice.blindfold_secret_info`

Required:

- `location` (String) Location Location is the uri_ref. It could be in url format for string:/// Or it could be a path if the store provider is an http/https location

Optional:

- `decryption_provider` (String) Decryption Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the backend Secret Management service.
- `store_provider` (String) Store Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_cookies_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice.clear_secret_info`

Required:

- `url` (String) URL URL of the secret. Currently supported URL schemes is string:///. For string:/// scheme, Secret needs to be encoded Base64 format. When asked for this secret, caller will get Secret bytes after Base64 decoding.

Optional:

- `provider` (String) Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///






<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_headers_to_add`

Required:

- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`
- `value_choice` (Attributes) value Enter Header value as string or blindfolded secret. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice))

Optional:

- `append` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_headers_to_add.value_choice`

Optional:

- `secret_value` (Attributes) Secret Value Secret Value of the HTTP header. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value))
- `value` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(8096)`

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_headers_to_add.value_choice.secret_value`

Required:

- `secret_info_choice` (Attributes) SecretInfoOneof Secret can be one of the following types - * Blindfold Secret - Secret is managed by F5XC Secret Management System * Clear Secret - Secret is not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_headers_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice`

Optional:

- `blindfold_secret_info` (Attributes) Blindfold Secret Blindfold Secret is used for the secrets managed by F5XC Secret Management Service (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--blindfold_secret_info))
- `clear_secret_info` (Attributes) Clear Secret Clear Secret is used for the secrets that are not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info))

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--blindfold_secret_info"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_headers_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice.blindfold_secret_info`

Required:

- `location` (String) Location Location is the uri_ref. It could be in url format for string:/// Or it could be a path if the store provider is an http/https location

Optional:

- `decryption_provider` (String) Decryption Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the backend Secret Management service.
- `store_provider` (String) Store Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--request_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.request_headers_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice.clear_secret_info`

Required:

- `url` (String) URL URL of the secret. Currently supported URL schemes is string:///. For string:/// scheme, Secret needs to be encoded Base64 format. When asked for this secret, caller will get Secret bytes after Base64 decoding.

Optional:

- `provider` (String) Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///






<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add`

Required:

- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`

Optional:

- `domain_choice` (Attributes) domain Domain to which cookie will be sent during request (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--domain_choice))
- `expiry_choice` (Attributes) Expiry Indicates the maximum lifetime of the cookie as an HTTP-date timestamp. Eg. Mon, 01 Dec 2008 00:23:53 GMT (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--expiry_choice))
- `httponly_choice` (Attributes) httponly A cookie with the HttpOnly attribute is inaccessible to the JavaScript. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--httponly_choice))
- `max_age_choice` (Attributes) max_age Enter the maximum age in minutes for session cookies after which cookies are automatically expired (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--max_age_choice))
- `overwrite` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `partitioned_choice` (Attributes) Partitioned A cookie with the Partitioned attribute is stored using partitioned storage. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--partitioned_choice))
- `path_choice` (Attributes) path The path that must exist in the requested URL for the browser to send the Cookie header. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--path_choice))
- `samesite_choice` (Attributes) samesite Controls whether or not a cookie is sent with cross-site requests (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--samesite_choice))
- `secure_choice` (Attributes) secure A cookie with the Secure attribute is only sent to the server with an encrypted request over the HTTPS protocol and therefore, is more resistant to man-in-the-middle attacks. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--secure_choice))
- `value_choice` (Attributes) value Enter Cookie value as string or blindfolded secret. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--domain_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.domain_choice`

Optional:

- `add_domain` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 256)`
- `ignore_domain` (Boolean) ignore_domain Ignore max age attribute


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--expiry_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.expiry_choice`

Optional:

- `add_expiry` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`
- `ignore_expiry` (Boolean) ignore_expiry Ignore expiry attribute


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--httponly_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.httponly_choice`

Optional:

- `add_httponly` (Boolean) add_httponly
- `ignore_httponly` (Boolean) ignore_httponly


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--max_age_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.max_age_choice`

Optional:

- `ignore_max_age` (Boolean) ignore_max_age Ignore max age attribute
- `max_age_value` (Number) <br>**Default:** `86400`


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--partitioned_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.partitioned_choice`

Optional:

- `add_partitioned` (Boolean) add_partitioned
- `ignore_partitioned` (Boolean) ignore_partitioned


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--path_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.path_choice`

Optional:

- `add_path` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`
- `ignore_path` (Boolean) ignore_domain Ignore path attribute


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--samesite_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.samesite_choice`

Optional:

- `ignore_samesite` (Boolean) ignore_samesite Ignore Samesite attribute
- `samesite_lax` (Boolean) lax Add Samesite attribute with Lax. Means that the cookie is not sent on cross-site requests
- `samesite_none` (Boolean) none Add Samesite attribute with None. Means that the browser sends the cookie with both cross-site and same-site requests
- `samesite_strict` (Boolean) strict Add Samesite attribute with Strict. Means that the browser sends the cookie only for same-site requests


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--secure_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.secure_choice`

Optional:

- `add_secure` (Boolean) add_secure
- `ignore_secure` (Boolean) ignore_secure


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.value_choice`

Optional:

- `ignore_value` (Boolean) ignore_value Ignore value of cookie
- `secret_value` (Attributes) Secret Value Secret Value of the Cookie header (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value))
- `value` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(8096)`

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.value_choice.secret_value`

Required:

- `secret_info_choice` (Attributes) SecretInfoOneof Secret can be one of the following types - * Blindfold Secret - Secret is managed by F5XC Secret Management System * Clear Secret - Secret is not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice`

Optional:

- `blindfold_secret_info` (Attributes) Blindfold Secret Blindfold Secret is used for the secrets managed by F5XC Secret Management Service (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--blindfold_secret_info))
- `clear_secret_info` (Attributes) Clear Secret Clear Secret is used for the secrets that are not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info))

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--blindfold_secret_info"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice.blindfold_secret_info`

Required:

- `location` (String) Location Location is the uri_ref. It could be in url format for string:/// Or it could be a path if the store provider is an http/https location

Optional:

- `decryption_provider` (String) Decryption Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the backend Secret Management service.
- `store_provider` (String) Store Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///


<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_cookies_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice--clear_secret_info"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_cookies_to_add.value_choice.secret_value.secret_info_choice.clear_secret_info`

Required:

- `url` (String) URL URL of the secret. Currently supported URL schemes is string:///. For string:/// scheme, Secret needs to be encoded Base64 format. When asked for this secret, caller will get Secret bytes after Base64 decoding.

Optional:

- `provider` (String) Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///






<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_headers_to_add"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_headers_to_add`

Required:

- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`
- `value_choice` (Attributes) value Enter Header value as string or blindfolded secret. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_headers_to_add--value_choice))

Optional:

- `append` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_headers_to_add--value_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_headers_to_add.value_choice`

Optional:

- `secret_value` (Attributes) Secret Value Secret Value of the HTTP header. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value))
- `value` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(8096)`

<a id="nestedatt--more_option--response_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value"></a>
### Nested Schema for `more_option.response_headers_to_add.value_choice.secret_value`

Required:

- `secret_info_choice` (Attributes) SecretInfoOneof Secret can be one of the following types - * Blindfold Secret - Secret is managed by F5XC Secret Management System * Clear Secret - Secret is not encrypted (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--more_option--response_headers_to_add--value_choice--secret_value--secret_info_choice))

- `decryption_provider` (String) Decryption Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the backend Secret Management service.
- `store_provider` (String) Store Provider Name of the Secret Management Access object that contains information about the store to get encrypted bytes This field needs to be provided only if the url scheme is not string:///

<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list`

Optional:

- `origin_server_subset_rules` (Attributes List) Origin Server Subset Origin Server Subset Rules allow users to define match condition on Client (IP address, ASN, Country), IP Reputation, Regional Edge names, Request for subset selection of origin servers. Origin Server Subset  is a sequential engine where rules are evaluated one after the other. It's important to define the correct order for Origin Server Subset  to get the intended result, rules are evaluated from top to bottom in the list. When an Origin server subset rule is matched, then this selection rule takes effect and no more rules are evaluated. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules))

<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules`

Required:

- `asn_choice` (Attributes) asn choice Match the 4-byte origin Autonomous System Number for the source IPv4 address of the request. The origin ASN for the request is obtained by performing a lookup for the source IPv4 Address in a GeoIP DB. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--asn_choice))
- `ip_choice` (Attributes) ip choice Match source IPv4 address of the request (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--ip_choice))
- `metadata` (Attributes) metadata Common attributes for the rule including name and description. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--metadata))
- `origin_server_subsets_action` (Map of String) Origin Server Labels Action Add labels to select one or more origin servers. Note: The pre-requisite settings to be configured in the origin pool are: 1. Add labels to origin servers 2. Enable subset load balancing in the Origin Server Subsets section and configure keys in origin server subsets classes
- `selector_choice` (Attributes) Client Selector choice Match based Label Selector Expressions (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--selector_choice))

Optional:

- `country_codes` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["COUNTRY_WF", "COUNTRY_IE", "COUNTRY_IS", "COUNTRY_TJ", "COUNTRY_TL", "COUNTRY_AL", "COUNTRY_HK", "COUNTRY_MN", "COUNTRY_UM", "COUNTRY_KW", <a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--asn_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.asn_choice`

Optional:

- `any_asn` (Boolean) any asn Any origin ASN.
- `asn_list` (Attributes) asn list List of 4-byte ASN values. The predicate evaluates to true if the origin ASN is present in the ASN list. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--asn_choice--asn_list))
- `asn_matcher` (Attributes) asn matcher List of references to BGP ASN Set objects. The predicate evaluates to true if the origin ASN is present in one of the BGP ASN Set objects. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--asn_choice--asn_matcher))

<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--asn_choice--asn_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.asn_choice.asn_list`

Required:

- `as_numbers` (List of Number) as numbers An unordered set of RFC 6793 defined 4-byte AS numbers that can be used to create allow or deny lists for use in network policy or service policy. It can be used to create the allow list only for DNS Load Balancer.


<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--asn_choice--asn_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.asn_choice.asn_matcher`

Required:

- `asn_sets` (Attributes List) asn_sets A list of references to bgp_asn_set objects. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--asn_choice--asn_matcher--asn_sets))

<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--asn_choice--asn_matcher--asn_sets"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.asn_choice.asn_matcher.asn_sets`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)




<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--ip_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.ip_choice`

Optional:

- `any_ip` (Boolean) any ip Any Source IP
- `ip_matcher` (Attributes) ip matcher List of references to IP Prefix Set objects. The predicate evaluates to true if the client IPv4 Address is covered by one or more of the IPv4 Prefixes in the IP Prefix Sets. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--ip_choice--ip_matcher))
- `ip_prefix_list` (Attributes) ip prefix list List of IPv4 Prefixes values. The predicate evaluates to true if the client IPv4 Address is covered by one or more of the IPv4 Prefixes from the list. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--ip_choice--ip_prefix_list))

<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--ip_choice--ip_matcher"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.ip_choice.ip_matcher`

Required:

- `prefix_sets` (Attributes List) prefix_sets A list of references to ip_prefix_set objects. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--ip_choice--ip_matcher--prefix_sets))

Optional:

- `invert_matcher` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`

<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--ip_choice--ip_matcher--prefix_sets"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.ip_choice.ip_matcher.prefix_sets`

Required:

- `name` (String)
- `namespace` (String)

Read-Only:

- `tenant` (String)



<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--ip_choice--ip_prefix_list"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.ip_choice.ip_prefix_list`

Optional:

- `invert_match` (Boolean) <br>**Default:** `false`
- `ip_prefixes` (List of String) ip prefixes List of IPv4 prefix strings.
- `ipv6_prefixes` (List of String) ipv6 prefixes List of IPv6 prefix strings.



<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--metadata"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.metadata`

Required:

- `name` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtLeast(1)`

Optional:

- `description` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(256)`


<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--selector_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.selector_choice`

Optional:

- `client_selector` (Attributes) client selector A label selector that describes the expected set of clients. The labels associated with the client making the API request are used to evaluate the label expressions in the selector. These labels can be derived from the client TLS certificate or from the volterra internal control plane. This is a more flexible and powerful version of the client name matcher predicate that allows a given rule to be applicable to a set of clients based on the client labels rather than being limited to relying on patterns in the client name. The predicate evaluates to true if the expressions in the label selector are true for the client labels. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--selector_choice--client_selector))
- `none` (Boolean) No Label Selector No Label Selector

<a id="nestedatt--origin_server_subset_rule_list--origin_server_subset_rules--selector_choice--client_selector"></a>
### Nested Schema for `origin_server_subset_rule_list.origin_server_subset_rules.selector_choice.client_selector`

Required:

- `expressions` (List of String) expressions expressions contains the kubernetes style label expression for selections.





<a id="nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules"></a>
### Nested Schema for `sensitive_data_disclosure_rules`

Optional:

- `sensitive_data_types_in_response` (Attributes List) Sensitive Data Exposure Rules Sensitive Data Exposure Rules allows specifying rules to mask sensitive data fields in API responses (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules--sensitive_data_types_in_response))

<a id="nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules--sensitive_data_types_in_response"></a>
### Nested Schema for `sensitive_data_disclosure_rules.sensitive_data_types_in_response`

Required:

- `type_condition_type_choice` (Attributes) condition type choices Choose the granularity of the rule (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules--sensitive_data_types_in_response--type_condition_type_choice))

Optional:

- `body` (Attributes) Body Section Masking Options (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules--sensitive_data_types_in_response--body))

<a id="nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules--sensitive_data_types_in_response--type_condition_type_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `sensitive_data_disclosure_rules.sensitive_data_types_in_response.type_condition_type_choice`

Optional:

- `api_endpoint` (Attributes) API Endpoint The API endpoint (Path + Method) which this validation applies to (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules--sensitive_data_types_in_response--type_condition_type_choice--api_endpoint))

<a id="nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules--sensitive_data_types_in_response--type_condition_type_choice--api_endpoint"></a>
### Nested Schema for `sensitive_data_disclosure_rules.sensitive_data_types_in_response.type_condition_type_choice.api_endpoint`

Required:

- `path` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthAtMost(1024)`

Optional:

- `methods` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["ANY", "GET", "HEAD", "PUT", "OPTIONS", "COPY", "POST", "DELETE", "CONNECT", "TRACE", "PATCH"]` (Multiple selection allowed)



<a id="nestedatt--sensitive_data_disclosure_rules--sensitive_data_types_in_response--body"></a>
### Nested Schema for `sensitive_data_disclosure_rules.sensitive_data_types_in_response.body`

Required:

- `fields` (List of String) Field Values List of JSON Path field values. Use square brackets with an underscore [_] to indicate array elements (e.g., person.emails[_]). To reference JSON keys that contain spaces, enclose the entire path in double quotes. For example: 'person.first name'.




<a id="nestedatt--waf_exclusion"></a>
### Nested Schema for `waf_exclusion`

Optional:

- `waf_exclusion_choice` (Attributes) WAF Exclusion Choice Define the Signatures, Violations, Attack Types, and Bot Names that should be excluded from WAF processing for HTTP requests matching specific domain, path, and method criteria. The WAF Exclusion is evaluated sequentially and can only be matched once per request. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `waf_exclusion.waf_exclusion_choice`

Optional:

- `waf_exclusion_inline_rules` (Attributes) Inline Rules An ordered list of rules specific to this Load Balancer. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules))
- `waf_exclusion_policy` (Attributes) Custom Policy A policy containing an ordered list of rules that can be applied to one or more Load Balancers or Routes. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_policy))

<a id="nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules"></a>
### Nested Schema for `waf_exclusion.waf_exclusion_choice.waf_exclusion_inline_rules`

Optional:

- `rules` (Attributes List) List of WAF Exclusion Rules An ordered list of WAF Exclusions specific to this Load Balancer. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules--rules))

<a id="nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules--rules"></a>
### Nested Schema for `waf_exclusion.waf_exclusion_choice.waf_exclusion_inline_rules.rules`

Required:

- `domain_choice` (Attributes) Domain Domain to be matched (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules--rules--domain_choice))
- `metadata` (Attributes) metadata Common attributes for the rule including name and description. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules--rules--metadata))
- `path_choice` (Attributes) Path Path to be matched (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules--rules--path_choice))

Optional:

- `expiration_timestamp` (String) expiration timestamp The expiration_timestamp is the RFC 3339 format timestamp at which the containing rule is considered to be logically expired. The rule continues to exist in the configuration but is not applied anymore.
- `methods` (List of String) <br>**Valid Options:** `["POST", "DELETE", "CONNECT", "TRACE", "PATCH", "ANY", "GET", "HEAD", "PUT", "OPTIONS", "COPY"]` (Multiple selection allowed)
- `waf_advanced_configuration_choice` (Attributes) WAF Exclusion Rule Action WAF Exclusion Rule action to be enforced if the input request matches the rule. (see [below for nested schema](#nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules--rules--waf_advanced_configuration_choice))

<a id="nestedatt--waf_exclusion--waf_exclusion_choice--waf_exclusion_inline_rules--rules--domain_choice"></a>
### Nested Schema for `waf_exclusion.waf_exclusion_choice.waf_exclusion_inline_rules.rules.domain_choice`

Optional:

- `any_domain` (Boolean) Any domain Apply this WAF exclusion rule for any domain
- `exact_value` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 256)`
- `suffix_value` (String) <br>**Validation:** `Length LengthBetween(1, 256)`

## Import

Import is supported using the following syntax:

```shell
terraform import random_string.test test
```

### Limitations of Import

Any attribute values that are specified within Terraform config will be
ignored during import and all attributes that have defaults defined within
the schema will have the default assigned.

For instance, using the following config during import:
```terraform
resource "random_string" "test" {
  length = 16
  lower  = false
}
```

Then importing the resource using `terraform import random_string.test test`,
would result in the triggering of a replacement (i.e., destroy-create) during
the next `terraform apply`.

### Avoiding Replacement

If the resource were imported using `terraform import random_string.test test`,
replacement can be avoided by using:

1. Attribute values that match the imported ID and defaults:
    ```terraform
    resource "random_string" "test" {
      length = 4
      lower  = true
    }
    ```

2. Attribute values that match the imported ID and omit the attributes with defaults:
    ```terraform
    resource "random_string" "test" {
      length = 4
    }
    ```

3. `ignore_changes` specifying the attributes to ignore:
    ```terraform
    resource "random_string" "test" {
      length = 16
      lower  = false

      lifecycle {
        ignore_changes = [
          length,
          lower,
        ]
      }
    }
    ```

    **NOTE** `ignore_changes` is only required until the resource is recreated after import,
    after which it will use the configuration values specified.
